======================================================
 13. PHP KAYNAK DOSYALARI - INCELEME ICIN

========== DOSYA: index.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 28800,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

$error = '';
$loggedIn = !empty($_SESSION['admin_id']);

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');
    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false
        ]
    );
} catch (Throwable $ex) {
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantısı kurulamadı. Sunucu yapılandırmasını kontrol edin.');
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $token = [GIZLENDI]

    if (!is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
        $error = 'Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız. Sayfayı yenileyin.';
    } elseif (isset($_POST['logout'])) {
        $_SESSION = [];
        session_destroy();
        header('Location: /');
        exit;
    } elseif (isset($_POST['login'])) {
        $username = trim((string)($_POST['username'] ?? ''));
        $password = [GIZLENDI]

        $stmt = [GIZLENDI]
        $stmt->execute([$username]);
        $admin = $stmt->fetch();

        if ($admin && password_verify($password, $admin['password_hash'])) {
            session_regenerate_id(true);
            $_SESSION['admin_id'] = (int)$admin['id'];
            $_SESSION['admin_username'] = $admin['username'];
            $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
            header('Location: /');
            exit;
        }

        $error = 'Kullanıcı adı veya şifre hatalı.';
    }
}

$loggedIn = !empty($_SESSION['admin_id']);

$stats = [
    'customers' => 0,
    'streams' => 0,
    'categories' => 0,
    'connections' => 0,
    'customer_active' => 0,
    'customer_expired' => 0,
    'customer_disabled' => 0,
    'live' => 0,
    'movie' => 0,
    'series' => 0
];

if ($loggedIn) {
    try {
        $stats['customers'] = (int)$pdo->query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers')->fetchColumn();
        $stats['streams'] = (int)$pdo->query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM streams')->fetchColumn();
        $typeCounts = $pdo->query("SELECT
            COALESCE(SUM(type='live'),0) AS live,
            COALESCE(SUM(type='movie'),0) AS movie,
            COALESCE(SUM(type='series'),0) AS series
            FROM streams")->fetch();
        $stats['live'] = (int)$typeCounts['live'];
        $stats['movie'] = (int)$typeCounts['movie'];
        $stats['series'] = (int)$typeCounts['series'];
        $stats['categories'] = (int)$pdo->query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM categories')->fetchColumn();
        $stats['connections'] = (int)$pdo->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stream_sessions WHERE revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW() AND last_seen >= NOW() - INTERVAL 2 MINUTE")->fetchColumn();
        $customerStatus = $pdo->query("
            SELECT
                COALESCE(SUM(status = 'active' AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > NOW())), 0) AS active_count,
                COALESCE(SUM(status = 'active' AND expires_at IS NOT NULL AND expires_at <= NOW()), 0) AS expired_count,
                COALESCE(SUM(status = 'disabled'), 0) AS disabled_count
            FROM customers
        ")->fetch();

        $stats['customer_active'] = (int)$customerStatus['active_count'];
        $stats['customer_expired'] = (int)$customerStatus['expired_count'];
        $stats['customer_disabled'] = (int)$customerStatus['disabled_count'];

    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        // İstatistik sorgusu başarısızsa panel çalışmaya devam eder.
    }
}
?>

<script>
function openMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('overlay').classList.add('show');
}
function closeMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('show');
}
document.querySelectorAll('.nav a').forEach(function(link){
    link.addEventListener('click',function(){
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav a').forEach(function(item){item.classList.remove('active')});
        this.classList.add('active');
        if(window.innerWidth<=760) closeMenu();
    });
});
</script>

========== DOSYA SONU: index.php ==========

========== DOSYA: admin_auth.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * Xtream Panel - Merkezi yönetici yetkilendirme sistemi.
 * Yetki kaydı varsa izin verilir; kayıt yoksa reddedilir.
 */

function admin_auth_current(PDO $pdo): ?array
{
    if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
        return null;
    }

    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
        'SELECT id, username, is_super_admin, is_active
         FROM admins
         WHERE id = ?
         LIMIT 1'
    );
    $stmt->execute([(int)$_SESSION['admin_id']]);
    $admin = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

    if (!$admin || (int)$admin['is_active'] !== 1) {
        $_SESSION = [];

        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
            session_destroy();
        }

        header('Location: /');
        exit;
    }

    return $admin;
}

function admin_auth_can(PDO $pdo, string $permission): bool
{
    $admin = admin_auth_current($pdo);

    if (!$admin) {
        return false;
    }

    if ((int)$admin['is_super_admin'] === 1) {
        return true;
    }

    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
        'SELECT 1
         FROM admin_permissions
         WHERE admin_id = ? AND permission_key = ?
         LIMIT 1'
    );
    $stmt->execute([(int)$admin['id'], $permission]);

    return (bool)$stmt->fetchColumn();
}

function admin_auth_require(PDO $pdo, string $permission): void
{
    if (!admin_auth_can($pdo, $permission)) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Bu işlem için yetkiniz bulunmuyor.');
    }
}

function admin_auth_require_super(PDO $pdo): void
{
    $admin = admin_auth_current($pdo);

    if (!$admin || (int)$admin['is_super_admin'] !== 1) {
        http_response_code(403);
        exit('Bu alana yalnızca ana yönetici erişebilir.');
    }
}

function admin_auth_require_any(PDO $pdo, array $permissions): void
{
    foreach ($permissions as $permission) {
        if (admin_auth_can($pdo, (string)$permission)) {
            return;
        }
    }

    http_response_code(403);
    exit('Bu sayfaya erişim yetkiniz bulunmuyor.');
}

========== DOSYA SONU: admin_auth.php ==========

========== DOSYA: customers.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 0,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($v): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$v, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');
    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false
        ]
    );
} catch (Throwable $ex) {
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantı hatası.');
}


    $pdo->exec("
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscription_renewals (
            id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
            customer_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            days_added SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
            old_expiry DATETIME NOT NULL,
            new_expiry DATETIME NOT NULL,
            admin_username VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL,
            created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (id),
            KEY idx_renewal_customer (customer_id, created_at),
            CONSTRAINT fk_renewal_customer
                FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES customers(id)
                ON DELETE CASCADE
        ) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4
    ");

function go(string $url = 'customers.php'): never {
    header('Location: ' . $url);
    exit;
}

function validDate(string $value): ?string {
    if ($value === '') return null;
    $date = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d\TH:i', $value);
    if (!$date || $date->format('Y-m-d\TH:i') !== $value) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz abonelik tarihi.');
    }
    return $date->format('Y-m-d H:i:s');
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $token = [GIZLENDI]

    if (!is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız. Sayfayı yenileyin.'];
        go();
    }

    $action = (string)($_POST['action'] ?? '');

    try {
        if ($action === 'create' || $action === 'update') {
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $username = trim((string)($_POST['username'] ?? ''));
            $password = [GIZLENDI]
            $status = (string)($_POST['status'] ?? 'active');
            $maxConnections = filter_var(
                $_POST['max_connections'] ?? null,
                FILTER_VALIDATE_INT
            );
            $expiresAt = validDate(trim((string)($_POST['expires_at'] ?? '')));
            $adultEnabled = (($_POST['adult_enabled'] ?? '0') === '1') ? 1 : 0;
            $packageInput = trim((string)($_POST['package_id'] ?? ($action === 'create' ? '1' : '')));
            $packageId = null;

            if ($packageInput !== '') {
                $packageId = filter_var($packageInput, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
                if ($packageId === false || $packageId < 1) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz paket seçimi.');
                }

                $packageCheck = $pdo->prepare('SELECT status FROM packages WHERE id=? LIMIT 1');
                $packageCheck->execute([$packageId]);
                $packageStatus = $packageCheck->fetchColumn();

                if ($packageStatus === false) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen paket bulunamadı.');
                }
                if ($action === 'create' && $packageStatus !== 'active') {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Pasif paketle müşteri oluşturulamaz.');
                }
            }

            if (strlen($username) < 3 || strlen($username) > 60) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Kullanıcı adı 3-60 karakter arasında olmalı.');
            }

            if (!preg_match('/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$/', $username)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Kullanıcı adında yalnızca harf, rakam, nokta, tire ve alt çizgi kullanın.');
            }

            if (!in_array($status, ['active', 'disabled'], true)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz hesap durumu.');
            }

            if ($maxConnections === false || $maxConnections < 1 || $maxConnections > 100) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Bağlantı limiti 1-100 arasında olmalı.');
            }

            if ($action === 'create') {
                if (strlen($password) < 8) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Yeni müşteri şifresi en az 8 karakter olmalı.');
                }

                $hash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

                $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                    'INSERT INTO customers (username, password_hash, status, expires_at, max_connections, package_id, adult_enabled)
                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
                );
                $stmt->execute([$username, $hash, $status, $expiresAt, $maxConnections, $packageId, $adultEnabled]);

                // XTREAM_CUSTOMER_TELEGRAM_NOTIFICATION
                try {
                    $telegram = @parse_ini_file(
                        '/etc/xtream-panel/telegram.ini',
                        false,
                        INI_SCANNER_RAW
                    );

                    if ($telegram && !empty($telegram['BOT_TOKEN'])) {
                        $expiryText = $expiresAt
                            ? date('d.m.Y H:i', strtotime($expiresAt))
                            : 'Sınırsız';

                        $groupMessage =
                            "🆕 YENİ XTREAM MÜŞTERİSİ\n\n" .
                            "👤 Kullanıcı: " . $username . "\n" .
                            "📅 Bitiş: " . $expiryText . "\n" .
                            "📱 Bağlantı limiti: " . $maxConnections . "\n" .
                            "🟢 Durum: " . $status;

                        $m3uUrl = 'https://xtream.mutluapk.xyz/get.php?username=' .
                            rawurlencode($username) .
                            '&password= [GIZLENDI]
                            '&type=m3u_plus';

                        $privateMessage =
                            "🔐 YENİ MÜŞTERİ GİRİŞ BİLGİLERİ\n\n" .
                            "👤 Kullanıcı adı: " . $username . "\n" .
                            "🔑 Şifre: " . $password . "\n" .
                            "📅 Bitiş: " . $expiryText . "\n" .
                            "📱 Bağlantı limiti: " . $maxConnections . "\n\n" .
                            "📺 M3U PLUS LİNKİ:\n" . $m3uUrl;

                        $sendTelegram = static function (
                            string $chatId,
                            string $message
                        ) use ($telegram): bool {
                            $ch = curl_init(
                                'https://api.telegram.org/bot' .
                                $telegram['BOT_TOKEN'] . '/sendMessage'
                            );

                            curl_setopt_array($ch, [
                                CURLOPT_POST => true,
                                CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => [
                                    'chat_id' => $chatId,
                                    'text' => $message
                                ],
                                CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
                                CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => 3,
                                CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 5,
                                CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER => true,
                                CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST => 2
                            ]);

                            $response = curl_exec($ch);
                            $error = curl_error($ch);
                            curl_close($ch);

                            $result = json_decode((string)$response, true);

                            if (!empty($result['ok'])) {
                                return true;
                            }

                            error_log(
                                'Xtream Telegram notification failed: ' .
                                ($error ?: ($result['description'] ?? 'Unknown error'))
                            );
                            return false;
                        };

                        if (!empty($telegram['CHAT_ID'])) {
                            $sendTelegram(
                                (string)$telegram['CHAT_ID'],
                                $groupMessage
                            );
                        }

                        if (!empty($telegram['PRIVATE_CHAT_ID'])) {
                            $sendTelegram(
                                (string)$telegram['PRIVATE_CHAT_ID'],
                                $privateMessage
                            );
                        } else {
                            error_log('Xtream Telegram private chat ID is missing.');
                        }
                    } else {
                        error_log('Xtream Telegram configuration is missing.');
                    }
                } catch (Throwable $telegramError) {
                    error_log(
                        'Xtream customer Telegram notification: ' .
                        $telegramError->getMessage()
                    );
                }

                $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Yeni müşteri başarıyla oluşturuldu.'];
                go();
            }

            if ($id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Müşteri kaydı bulunamadı.');
            }

            if ($password != [GIZLENDI]
                if (strlen($password) < 8) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Yeni şifre en az 8 karakter olmalı.');
                }

                $hash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
                $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE customers
                     SET username= [GIZLENDI]
                     WHERE id=?'
                );
                $stmt->execute([$username, $hash, $status, $expiresAt, $maxConnections, $packageId, $adultEnabled, $id]);
            } else {
                $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE customers
                     SET username=?, status=?, expires_at=?, max_connections=?, package_id=?, adult_enabled=?
                     WHERE id=?'
                );
                $stmt->execute([$username, $status, $expiresAt, $maxConnections, $packageId, $adultEnabled, $id]);
            }

            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Müşteri bilgileri güncellendi.'];
            go();
        }


        // XTREAM_SUBSCRIPTION_RENEWAL_SYSTEM
        if ($action === 'renew') {
            $id = filter_var($_POST['id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
            $days = filter_var($_POST['days'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

            if (!$id || $id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz müşteri kaydı.');
            }

            if (!in_array($days, [30, 90, 180, 365], true)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz yenileme süresi.');
            }

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            try {
                $lock = $pdo->prepare(
                    'SELECT id, username, status, expires_at
                     FROM customers WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE'
                );
                $lock->execute([$id]);
                $customer = $lock->fetch();

                if (!$customer) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Müşteri bulunamadı.');
                }

                if ($customer['expires_at'] === null) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Sınırsız aboneliğe süre eklenemez.');
                }

                $oldExpiry = new DateTimeImmutable(
                    $customer['expires_at'],
                    new DateTimeZone('UTC')
                );

                $now = new DateTimeImmutable('now', new DateTimeZone('UTC'));
                $base = $oldExpiry > $now ? $oldExpiry : $now;
                $newExpiry = $base->modify('+' . $days . ' days');

                $newExpiryText = $newExpiry->format('Y-m-d H:i:s');

                $update = $pdo->prepare(
                    'UPDATE customers SET expires_at = ? WHERE id = ?'
                );
                $update->execute([$newExpiryText, $id]);

                $history = $pdo->prepare(
                    'INSERT INTO subscription_renewals
                     (customer_id, days_added, old_expiry, new_expiry, admin_username)
                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
                );
                $history->execute([
                    $id,
                    $days,
                    $customer['expires_at'],
                    $newExpiryText,
                    (string)($_SESSION['admin_username'] ?? 'Admin')
                ]);

                $pdo->commit();

                $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                    'success',
                    $customer['username'] . ' hesabına ' . $days .
                    ' gün eklendi. Yeni bitiş: ' .
                    $newExpiry->format('d.m.Y H:i')
                ];

                // Telegram bildirimi; başarısız olsa da yenileme kaydı korunur.
                try {
                    $telegram = @parse_ini_file(
                        '/etc/xtream-panel/telegram.ini',
                        false,
                        INI_SCANNER_RAW
                    );

                    if ($telegram && !empty($telegram['BOT_TOKEN']) &&
                        !empty($telegram['CHAT_ID'])) {

                        $message =
                            "🔄 XTREAM ABONELİK YENİLENDİ\n\n" .
                            "👤 Müşteri: " . $customer['username'] . "\n" .
                            "➕ Eklenen süre: " . $days . " gün\n" .
                            "📅 Eski bitiş: " .
                            $oldExpiry->format('d.m.Y H:i') . "\n" .
                            "🗓 Yeni bitiş: " .
                            $newExpiry->format('d.m.Y H:i') . "\n" .
                            "🛡 İşlemi yapan: " .
                            (string)($_SESSION['admin_username'] ?? 'Admin') .
                            "\n\nZEM MEDYA • XTREAM CONTROL";

                        $ch = curl_init(
                            'https://api.telegram.org/bot' .
                            $telegram['BOT_TOKEN'] . '/sendMessage'
                        );

                        curl_setopt_array($ch, [
                            CURLOPT_POST => true,
                            CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => [
                                'chat_id' => $telegram['CHAT_ID'],
                                'text' => $message,
                                'disable_web_page_preview' => true
                            ],
                            CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
                            CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => 3,
                            CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 5,
                            CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER => true,
                            CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST => 2
                        ]);

                        $response = curl_exec($ch);
                        $error = curl_error($ch);
                        curl_close($ch);

                        $result = json_decode((string)$response, true);

                        if (empty($result['ok'])) {
                            error_log(
                                'Xtream renewal Telegram error: ' .
                                ($error ?: ($result['description'] ?? 'Unknown error'))
                            );
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Throwable $telegramError) {
                    error_log(
                        'Xtream renewal Telegram notification: ' .
                        $telegramError->getMessage()
                    );
                }

                go();
            } catch (Throwable $ex) {
                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $pdo->rollBack();
                }
                throw $ex;
            }
        }

        if ($action === 'toggle') {
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE customers
                 SET status = IF(status = 'active', 'disabled', 'active')
                 WHERE id = ?"
            );
            $stmt->execute([$id]);

            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Müşteri hesap durumu değiştirildi.'];
            go();
        }

        if ($action === 'delete') {
            $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM customers WHERE id = ?');
            $stmt->execute([$id]);

            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Müşteri kaydı silindi.'];
            go();
        }

        if ($action === 'logout') {
            $_SESSION = [];
            session_destroy();
            go('/');
        }

        throw new RuntimeException('Bilinmeyen işlem.');
    } catch (PDOException $ex) {
        if ((string)$ex->getCode() === '23000') {
            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Bu kullanıcı adı zaten kayıtlı. Başka bir kullanıcı adı deneyin.'];
        } else {
            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Veritabanı işlemi tamamlanamadı.'];
        }
        go();
    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', $ex->getMessage()];
        go();
    }
}

$packages = $pdo->query("SELECT id, name, status FROM packages ORDER BY sort_order, id")->fetchAll();

$flash = $_SESSION['flash'] ?? null;
unset($_SESSION['flash']);

$edit = null;
$editId = filter_input(INPUT_GET, 'edit', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

if ($editId) {
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id, username, status, expires_at, max_connections, package_id, adult_enabled FROM customers WHERE id=?');
    $stmt->execute([$editId]);
    $edit = $stmt->fetch() ?: null;
}

$search = trim((string)($_GET['q'] ?? ''));

if ($search !== '') {
    $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
        'SELECT c.id, c.username, c.status, c.expires_at, c.max_connections,
                c.created_at, c.adult_enabled, c.package_id, p.name AS package_name
         FROM customers c
         LEFT JOIN packages p ON p.id = c.package_id
         WHERE c.username LIKE ? ORDER BY c.id DESC LIMIT 200'
    );
    $stmt->execute(['%' . $search . '%']);
    $customers = $stmt->fetchAll();
} else {
    $customers = $pdo->query(
        'SELECT c.id, c.username, c.status, c.expires_at, c.max_connections,
                c.created_at, c.adult_enabled, c.package_id, p.name AS package_name
         FROM customers c
         LEFT JOIN packages p ON p.id = c.package_id
         ORDER BY c.id DESC LIMIT 200'
    )->fetchAll();
}

$total = (int)$pdo->query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers')->fetchColumn();
$active = (int)$pdo->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers WHERE status='active' AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > NOW())")->fetchColumn();
$disabled = (int)$pdo->query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customers WHERE status='disabled'")->fetchColumn();

// XTREAM_SUBSCRIPTION_RENEWAL_SYSTEM
$renewalHistory = $pdo->query("
    SELECT r.id, r.customer_id, r.days_added, r.old_expiry,
           r.new_expiry, r.admin_username, r.created_at, c.username
    FROM subscription_renewals r
    INNER JOIN customers c ON c.id = r.customer_id
    ORDER BY r.id DESC
    LIMIT 50
")->fetchAll();


function dateInput($value): string {
    if (!$value) return '';
    return date('Y-m-d\TH:i', strtotime($value));
}

                        $now = time();
                        $expiryTimestamp = !empty($customer['expires_at']) ? strtotime($customer['expires_at']) : null;
                        $isDisabled = $customer['status'] !== 'active';
                        $expired = !$isDisabled && $expiryTimestamp !== null && $expiryTimestamp <= $now;
                        $remainingSeconds = (!$isDisabled && $expiryTimestamp !== null && !$expired)
                            ? $expiryTimestamp - $now : null;
                        $remainingDays = $remainingSeconds !== null ? (int)ceil($remainingSeconds / 86400) : null;
                        $expiringSoon = !$isDisabled && !$expired && $remainingDays !== null && $remainingDays <= 7;
                    ?>
                    <tr>

                                <?php endif; ?>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </td>
                        <td>

                                <span class="subtext">Sınırsız</span>
                            <?php else: ?>
                                <?=e(date('d.m.Y H:i', $expiryTimestamp))?>
                                <?php if ($expired): ?>
                                    <span class="remaining-days expired">Abonelik süresi doldu</span>
                                <?php elseif (!$isDisabled && $remainingSeconds !== null): ?>
                                    <span class="remaining-days">
                                        Kalan: <?=$remainingSeconds < 86400 ? '24 saatten az' : $remainingDays . ' gün'?>
                                    </span>
                                <?php endif; ?>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </td>
                        <td><?=e($customer['package_name'] ?? 'Paketsiz')?></td>
                        <td>
                            <?php if ((int)($customer['adult_enabled'] ?? 0) === 1): ?>
                                <span class="status active">● Açık</span>
                            <?php else: ?>
                                <span class="status disabled">● Kapalı</span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </td>
                        <td><?= (int)$customer['max_connections'] ?> cihaz</td>
                        <td><?=e(date('d.m.Y', strtotime($customer['created_at'])))?></td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="actions">
                                
                                <form class="renew-controls" method="post"
                                      onsubmit="return confirm('Bu müşterinin aboneliğine seçilen süre eklensin mi?')">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="<?=e($_SESSION['csrf'])?>">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="action" value="renew">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="<?=(int)$customer['id']?>">
                                    <select name="days" aria-label="Yenileme süresi">

                                    </select>
                                    <button class="renew-btn" type="submit">＋ Uzat</button>

                                </form>
                                <form method="post" onsubmit="return confirm('Bu müşteri kalıcı olarak silinsin mi?')">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="csrf" value="<?=e($_SESSION['csrf'])?>">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="<?=(int)$customer['id']?>">
                                    <button class="small-btn red" name="action" value="delete" type="submit">Sil</button>
                                </form>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                <?php endif; ?>
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class="panel" id="renewal-history">

function openMenu(){
 document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
 document.getElementById('overlay').classList.add('show');
}
function closeMenu(){
 document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
 document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('show');
}

========== DOSYA SONU: customers.php ==========

========== DOSYA: connections.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 0,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($v): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$v, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');
    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false
        ]
    );
} catch (Throwable $ex) {
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantısı kurulamadı.');
}

function go(): never {
    header('Location: /connections.php');
    exit;
}

function deviceInfo(string $ua): array {
    $device = 'Bilinmeyen cihaz';
    $browser = 'Bilinmeyen tarayıcı';

    if (stripos($ua, 'TelegramBot') !== false) {
        return ['Telegram bağlantı önizlemesi', 'TelegramBot'];
    }

    if (stripos($ua, 'Android') !== false) {
        $device = 'Android cihaz';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'iPhone') !== false) {
        $device = 'iPhone';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'iPad') !== false) {
        $device = 'iPad';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Windows') !== false) {
        $device = 'Windows bilgisayar';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Macintosh') !== false || stripos($ua, 'Mac OS') !== false) {
        $device = 'Mac bilgisayar';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'SmartTV') !== false || stripos($ua, 'SMART-TV') !== false) {
        $device = 'Smart TV';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Linux') !== false) {
        $device = 'Linux cihaz';
    }

    if (stripos($ua, 'Edg/') !== false) {
        $browser = 'Microsoft Edge';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'OPR/') !== false) {
        $browser = 'Opera';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Firefox/') !== false) {
        $browser = 'Firefox';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Chrome/') !== false) {
        $browser = 'Google Chrome';
    } elseif (stripos($ua, 'Safari/') !== false) {
        $browser = 'Safari';
    }

    return [$device, $browser];
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $csrf = $_POST['csrf'] ?? '';

    if (!is_string($csrf) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'], $csrf)) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.'];
        go();
    }

    $action = (string)($_POST['action'] ?? '');

    try {
        if ($action === 'disconnect') {
            $id = filter_var($_POST['session_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

            if (!$id || $id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz oturum numarası.');
            }

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE stream_sessions
                 SET revoked_at=NOW()
                 WHERE id=? AND revoked_at IS NULL"
            );
            $stmt->execute([$id]);

            $pdo->prepare(
                "DELETE FROM stream_resources WHERE session_id=?"
            )->execute([$id]);

            $pdo->commit();

            $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                'success',
                $stmt->rowCount() ? 'Cihaz bağlantısı iptal edildi.' : 'Oturum zaten kapatılmış.'
            ];

        } elseif ($action === 'ban_device') {
            $id = filter_var($_POST['session_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
            if (!$id || $id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz oturum numarası.');
            }

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            $find = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT customer_id, device_hash, ip_address, user_agent
                 FROM stream_sessions WHERE id=? LIMIT 1"
            );
            $find->execute([$id]);
            $device = $find->fetch();

            if (!$device) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Cihaz kaydı bulunamadı.');
            }

            $add = $pdo->prepare(
                "INSERT INTO device_blacklist
                 (customer_id, device_hash, ip_address, user_agent, blocked_at)
                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, NOW())
                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                   ip_address=VALUES(ip_address),
                   user_agent=VALUES(user_agent),
                   blocked_at=NOW()"
            );
            $add->execute([
                (int)$device['customer_id'],
                $device['device_hash'],
                $device['ip_address'],
                $device['user_agent']
            ]);

            $pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE stream_sessions
                 SET revoked_at=COALESCE(revoked_at,NOW())
                 WHERE customer_id=? AND device_hash=?"
            )->execute([
                (int)$device['customer_id'],
                $device['device_hash']
            ]);

            $pdo->prepare(
                "DELETE sr FROM stream_resources sr
                 INNER JOIN stream_sessions ss ON ss.id=sr.session_id
                 WHERE ss.customer_id=? AND ss.device_hash=?"
            )->execute([
                (int)$device['customer_id'],
                $device['device_hash']
            ]);

            $pdo->commit();
            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Cihaz kalıcı olarak kara listeye alındı.'];

        } elseif ($action === 'unban_device') {
            $id = filter_var($_POST['blacklist_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
            if (!$id || $id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz kara liste kaydı.');
            }

            $remove = $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM device_blacklist WHERE id=?");
            $remove->execute([$id]);

            $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                'success',
                $remove->rowCount()
                    ? 'Cihazın engeli kaldırıldı. Yeniden bağlanabilir.'
                    : 'Kara liste kaydı bulunamadı.'
            ];

        } elseif ($action === 'disconnect_customer') {
            $customerId = filter_var($_POST['customer_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

            if (!$customerId || $customerId < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz müşteri numarası.');
            }

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            $pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE stream_sessions
                 SET revoked_at=NOW()
                 WHERE customer_id=? AND revoked_at IS NULL"
            )->execute([$customerId]);

            $pdo->prepare(
                "DELETE sr FROM stream_resources sr
                 INNER JOIN stream_sessions ss ON ss.id=sr.session_id
                 WHERE ss.customer_id=?"
            )->execute([$customerId]);

            $pdo->commit();

            $_SESSION['flash'] = ['success', 'Müşterinin tüm oturumları iptal edildi.'];

        } else {
            throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz işlem.');
        }
    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
            $pdo->rollBack();
        }

        error_log('Connections panel: ' . $ex->getMessage());
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'İşlem gerçekleştirilemedi.'];
    }

    go();
}

$flash = $_SESSION['flash'] ?? null;
unset($_SESSION['flash']);

$stats = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total,
            COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id) AS customers
     FROM stream_sessions
     WHERE revoked_at IS NULL
       AND expires_at>NOW()
       AND last_seen>=DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 2 MINUTE)"
)->fetch();

$stmt = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT ss.id, ss.customer_id, ss.ip_address, ss.user_agent,
            ss.current_stream_type, ss.current_stream_id,
            ss.created_at, ss.last_seen, ss.expires_at,
            c.username, c.max_connections,
            COALESCE(
                st.name,
                CONCAT(sr.name, ' / ', ep.name),
                CASE
                    WHEN ss.current_stream_type IS NULL THEN 'Şu anda yayın bilgisi yok'
                    ELSE 'Yayın bilgisi bulunamadı'
                END
            ) AS current_title
     FROM stream_sessions ss
     INNER JOIN customers c ON c.id=ss.customer_id
     LEFT JOIN streams st
       ON st.id=ss.current_stream_id
      AND ss.current_stream_type IN ('live','movie')
     LEFT JOIN episodes ep
       ON ep.id=ss.current_stream_id
      AND ss.current_stream_type='series'
     LEFT JOIN seasons se ON se.id=ep.season_id
     LEFT JOIN streams sr ON sr.id=se.series_id
     WHERE ss.revoked_at IS NULL
       AND ss.expires_at>NOW()
       AND ss.last_seen>=DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 2 MINUTE)
     ORDER BY ss.last_seen DESC"
);


$sessions = $stmt->fetchAll();

$blacklist = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT b.id, b.ip_address, b.user_agent, b.blocked_at,
            b.customer_id, c.username
     FROM device_blacklist b
     INNER JOIN customers c ON c.id=b.customer_id
     ORDER BY b.blocked_at DESC"
)->fetchAll();
?>

    <?php if ($flash): ?>

========== DOSYA: streams.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 0,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('Content-Type: text/html; charset=UTF-8');
header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Cache-Control: no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate');

function h($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && ($_POST['action'] ?? '') === 'logout') {
    $token = [GIZLENDI]
    if (is_string($token) && hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
        $_SESSION = [];
        session_destroy();
    }
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

$counts = ['live' => 0, 'movie' => 0, 'series' => 0];
$active = 0;
$dbWarning = '';

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');

    if (!$config || empty($config['DB_HOST']) || empty($config['DB_NAME']) ||
        empty($config['DB_USER']) || !isset($config['DB_PASS'])) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Database configuration unavailable');
    }

    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_TIMEOUT => 5
        ]
    );

    $query = $pdo->query('SELECT type, COUNT(*) AS total FROM streams GROUP BY type');

    foreach ($query as $row) {
        $type = (string)($row['type'] ?? '');
        if (array_key_exists($type, $counts)) {
            $counts[$type] = (int)$row['total'];
        }
    }

    $active = (int)$pdo->query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM streams WHERE status = 'active'"
    )->fetchColumn();

} catch (Throwable $error) {
    $dbWarning = 'İçerik sayıları şu anda alınamadı. Yönetim bağlantıları kullanılabilir.';
}

$total = $counts['live'] + $counts['movie'] + $counts['series'];
$username = $_SESSION['admin_username'] ?? 'Admin';
?>

<script>
function openMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('menuOverlay').classList.add('show');
    document.body.classList.add('menu-open');
}
function closeMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('menuOverlay').classList.remove('show');
    document.body.classList.remove('menu-open');
}
document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    if(e.key==='Escape') closeMenu();
});
</script>

========== DOSYA SONU: streams.php ==========

========== DOSYA: packages.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 0,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

function go(string $url = '/packages.php'): never {
    header('Location: '.$url);
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file(
        '/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini',
        false,
        INI_SCANNER_RAW
    );

    if (!$config) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Veritabanı ayarı okunamadı.');
    }

    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false
        ]
    );
} catch (Throwable $ex) {
    error_log('Packages database error: '.$ex->getMessage());
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantı hatası.');
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $token = [GIZLENDI]

    if (!is_string($token) ||
        !hash_equals((string)$_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.'];
        go();
    }

    try {
        $action = (string)($_POST['action'] ?? '');

        if ($action === 'package_create') {
            $name = trim((string)($_POST['name'] ?? ''));
            $description = trim((string)($_POST['description'] ?? ''));
            $sortOrder = filter_var(
                $_POST['sort_order'] ?? 1,
                FILTER_VALIDATE_INT
            );

            if ($name === '' || mb_strlen($name) > 100) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paket adı 1-100 karakter olmalı.');
            }

            if (mb_strlen($description) > 255) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Açıklama en fazla 255 karakter olabilir.');
            }

            if ($sortOrder === false || $sortOrder < 0) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Sıralama değeri geçersiz.');
            }

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                "INSERT INTO packages (name,description,status,sort_order)
                 VALUES (?,?,'active',?)"
            );
            $stmt->execute([$name, $description, $sortOrder]);

            $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                'success',
                'Yeni paket oluşturuldu. Kategorilerini aşağıdan seçebilirsin.'
            ];
            go();
        }

        if ($action === 'package_save') {
            $id = filter_var($_POST['id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
            $name = trim((string)($_POST['name'] ?? ''));
            $description = trim((string)($_POST['description'] ?? ''));
            $status = (string)($_POST['status'] ?? '');
            $sortOrder = filter_var(
                $_POST['sort_order'] ?? 1,
                FILTER_VALIDATE_INT
            );

            if (!$id || $id < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paket numarası geçersiz.');
            }

            if ($name === '' || mb_strlen($name) > 100) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paket adı 1-100 karakter olmalı.');
            }

            if (mb_strlen($description) > 255) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Açıklama en fazla 255 karakter olabilir.');
            }

            if (!in_array($status, ['active', 'disabled'], true)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paket durumu geçersiz.');
            }

            if ($sortOrder === false || $sortOrder < 0) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Sıralama değeri geçersiz.');
            }

            $check = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM packages WHERE id=? LIMIT 1');
            $check->execute([$id]);

            if (!$check->fetchColumn()) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paket bulunamadı.');
            }

            $categoryIds = $_POST['category_ids'] ?? [];

            if (!is_array($categoryIds)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Kategori seçimi geçersiz.');
            }

            $categoryIds = array_values(array_unique(array_map(
                static function ($value): int {
                    $id = filter_var($value, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
                    if ($id === false || $id < 1) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz kategori numarası.');
                    }
                    return $id;
                },
                $categoryIds
            )));

            if (count($categoryIds) > 1000) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Çok fazla kategori seçildi.');
            }

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                'UPDATE packages
                 SET name=?, description=?, status=?, sort_order=?
                 WHERE id=?'
            );
            $stmt->execute([
                $name,
                $description,
                $status,
                $sortOrder,
                $id
            ]);

            $validIds = [];

            if ($categoryIds) {
                $placeholders = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($categoryIds), '?'));
                $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                    "SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id IN ($placeholders)"
                );
                $stmt->execute($categoryIds);
                $validIds = array_map(
                    'intval',
                    $stmt->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN)
                );

                if (count($validIds) !== count($categoryIds)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen kategorilerden bazıları bulunamadı.');
                }
            }

            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                'DELETE FROM package_categories WHERE package_id=?'
            );
            $stmt->execute([$id]);

            if ($validIds) {
                $insert = $pdo->prepare(
                    'INSERT INTO package_categories (package_id,category_id)
                     VALUES (?,?)'
                );

                foreach ($validIds as $categoryId) {
                    $insert->execute([$id, $categoryId]);
                }
            }

            $pdo->commit();

            $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                'success',
                'Paket bilgileri ve kategori yetkileri kaydedildi.'
            ];
            go();
        }

        throw new RuntimeException('Bilinmeyen işlem.');
    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
            $pdo->rollBack();
        }

        error_log('Package operation error: '.$ex->getMessage());

        $_SESSION['flash'] = [
            'error',
            $ex instanceof PDOException
                ? 'Veritabanı işlemi tamamlanamadı.'
                : $ex->getMessage()
        ];
        go();
    }
}

$flash = $_SESSION['flash'] ?? null;
unset($_SESSION['flash']);

$packages = $pdo->query(
    'SELECT * FROM packages ORDER BY sort_order,id'
)->fetchAll();

$categories = $pdo->query(
    'SELECT id,name,type,is_adult
     FROM categories
     ORDER BY FIELD(type,"live","movie","series"),sort_order,name'
)->fetchAll();

$assignments = [];
$stmt = $pdo->query(
    'SELECT package_id,category_id FROM package_categories'
);

foreach ($stmt->fetchAll() as $row) {
    $assignments[(int)$row['package_id']][] = (int)$row['category_id'];
}

$groupNames = [
    'live' => 'CANLI TV KATEGORİLERİ',
    'movie' => 'FİLM KATEGORİLERİ',
    'series' => 'DİZİ KATEGORİLERİ'
];

$grouped = [
    'live' => [],
    'movie' => [],
    'series' => []
];

foreach ($categories as $category) {
    $grouped[$category['type']][] = $category;
}

$activeCount = count(array_filter(
    $packages,
    static fn($p) => $p['status'] === 'active'
));

$categoryCount = count($categories);
$totalAssignments = (int)$pdo->query(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM package_categories'
)->fetchColumn();
?>

                </div>

                <div class="card-footer">
                    <small><?=count($selected)?> kategori atanmış</small>
                    <button class="button primary" type="submit">✓ Paketi ve Yetkileri Kaydet</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </section>
    <?php endforeach; ?>
    </div>
</div>
</main>

<script>
function openMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
    const overlay=document.getElementById('overlay');
    overlay.style.display='block';
    overlay.classList.add('show');
}
function closeMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
    const overlay=document.getElementById('overlay');
    overlay.classList.remove('show');
    overlay.style.display='none';
}
function setGroup(button,checked){
    const group=button.closest('.category-group');
    group.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(input=>{
        input.checked=checked;
    });
}
</script>

</html>

========== DOSYA SONU: packages.php ==========

========== DOSYA: epg.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime' => 0,
    'path' => '/',
    'secure' => true,
    'httponly' => true,
    'samesite' => 'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

function go(): never {
    header('Location: /epg.php');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    header('Location: /');
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

try {
    $config = parse_ini_file(
        '/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini',
        false,
        INI_SCANNER_RAW
    );

    if (!$config) {
        throw new RuntimeException('Veritabanı ayarı okunamadı.');
    }

    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],
        $config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES => false
        ]
    );
} catch (Throwable $ex) {
    error_log('EPG database error: '.$ex->getMessage());
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantı hatası.');
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $token = [GIZLENDI]

    if (!is_string($token) ||
        !hash_equals((string)$_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', 'Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.'];
        go();
    }

    try {
        $action = (string)($_POST['action'] ?? '');

        if ($action === 'map_save') {
            $streamId = filter_var(
                $_POST['stream_id'] ?? null,
                FILTER_VALIDATE_INT
            );

            $channelId = trim((string)($_POST['channel_id'] ?? ''));

            if ($streamId === false || $streamId === null || $streamId < 1) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Canlı yayın seçimi geçersiz.');
            }

            $check = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type='live' LIMIT 1"
            );
            $check->execute([$streamId]);

            if (!$check->fetchColumn()) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Canlı yayın bulunamadı.');
            }

            if ($channelId === '') {
                $delete = $pdo->prepare(
                    "DELETE FROM epg_stream_map WHERE stream_id=?"
                );
                $delete->execute([$streamId]);

                $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                    'success',
                    'Yayın için EPG eşleştirmesi kaldırıldı.'
                ];
                go();
            }

            $check = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT channel_id FROM epg_channels WHERE channel_id=? LIMIT 1"
            );
            $check->execute([$channelId]);

            if (!$check->fetchColumn()) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen EPG kanalı bulunamadı.');
            }

            $save = $pdo->prepare(
                "INSERT INTO epg_stream_map (stream_id,channel_id)
                 VALUES (?,?)
                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE channel_id=VALUES(channel_id)"
            );
            $save->execute([$streamId, $channelId]);

            $_SESSION['flash'] = [
                'success',
                'EPG kanal eşleştirmesi kaydedildi.'
            ];
            go();
        }

        throw new RuntimeException('Geçersiz işlem.');
    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        $_SESSION['flash'] = ['error', $ex->getMessage()];
        go();
    }
}

$flash = $_SESSION['flash'] ?? null;
unset($_SESSION['flash']);

$stats = [
    'channels' => (int)$pdo->query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM epg_channels"
    )->fetchColumn(),

    'programmes' => (int)$pdo->query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM epg_programmes"
    )->fetchColumn(),

    'mapped' => (int)$pdo->query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM epg_stream_map"
    )->fetchColumn()
];

$setting = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT * FROM epg_settings WHERE id=1 LIMIT 1"
)->fetch() ?: [];

$streams = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT s.id,s.name,m.channel_id,c.display_name
     FROM streams s
     LEFT JOIN epg_stream_map m ON m.stream_id=s.id
     LEFT JOIN epg_channels c ON c.channel_id=m.channel_id
     WHERE s.type='live'
     ORDER BY s.name"
)->fetchAll();

$channels = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT channel_id,display_name
     FROM epg_channels
     ORDER BY display_name"
)->fetchAll();

$programmes = $pdo->query(
    "SELECT
        s.id AS stream_id,
        s.name AS stream_name,
        c.display_name AS channel_name,
        p.title,
        p.description,
        p.start_time,
        p.stop_time,
        CASE
            WHEN p.start_time <= UTC_TIMESTAMP()
             AND (p.stop_time IS NULL OR p.stop_time > UTC_TIMESTAMP())
            THEN 'ŞİMDİ'
            ELSE 'SIRADAKİ'
        END AS programme_status
     FROM epg_stream_map m
     INNER JOIN streams s ON s.id=m.stream_id
     INNER JOIN epg_channels c ON c.channel_id=m.channel_id
     INNER JOIN epg_programmes p ON p.channel_id=m.channel_id
     WHERE s.type='live'
       AND p.start_time >= DATE_SUB(UTC_TIMESTAMP(), INTERVAL 6 HOUR)
       AND p.start_time <= DATE_ADD(UTC_TIMESTAMP(), INTERVAL 2 DAY)
     ORDER BY s.name,p.start_time
     LIMIT 150"
)->fetchAll();

function xtreamEpgFormatLocalTime($value): string {
    if (!$value) return '-';
    try {
        $date = new DateTimeImmutable($value, new DateTimeZone('UTC'));
        return $date->setTimezone(new DateTimeZone('Europe/Istanbul'))
            ->format('d.m.Y H:i');
    } catch (Throwable $e) {
        return '-';
    }
}

<script>
function openMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
    const overlay=document.getElementById('overlay');
    overlay.style.display='block';
    overlay.classList.add('show');
}
function closeMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
    const overlay=document.getElementById('overlay');
    overlay.classList.remove('show');
    overlay.style.display='none';
}

const channelSearch=document.getElementById('channelSearch');
const channelSelect=document.getElementById('channelSelect');

if(channelSearch && channelSelect){
    const allOptions=Array.from(channelSelect.options).map(option=>({
        value:option.value,
        text:option.text,
        original:option
    }));

    channelSearch.addEventListener('input',()=>{
        const term=channelSearch.value.toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR');
        const selected=channelSelect.value;

        channelSelect.innerHTML='';

        allOptions.forEach(item=>{
            if(
                item.value==='' ||
                item.text.toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR').includes(term)
            ){
                channelSelect.add(new Option(item.text,item.value));
            }
        });

        if(Array.from(channelSelect.options).some(o=>o.value===selected)){
            channelSelect.value=selected;
        }
    });
}

const streamSelect=document.getElementById('streamSelect');

if(streamSelect && channelSelect){
    streamSelect.addEventListener('change',()=>{
        const option=streamSelect.selectedOptions[0];
        const current=option?.dataset.channel || '';

        if(current){
            channelSelect.value=current;
        }else{
            channelSelect.value='';
        }
    });

    streamSelect.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
}

function normalizeLiveCategoryText(string $text): string {
    $text = mb_strtoupper($text, 'UTF-8');
    $text = strtr($text, [
        'İ'=>'I', 'Ş'=>'S', 'Ğ'=>'G',
        'Ü'=>'U', 'Ö'=>'O', 'Ç'=>'C'
    ]);
    $text = preg_replace('/[^A-Z0-9]+/', ' ', $text);
    return trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $text));
}

function guessLiveCategoryId(string $name, string $group, array $categories): int {
    $rules = [
        'Spor' => [
            'SPOR','BEIN','B EIN','S SPORT','SSPORT','EXXEN',
            'EUROSPORT','TRT SPOR','TIVIBU SPOR','NBA',
            'FORMULA','MOTOR SPOR','TJK','AT YARISI','GS TV',
            'FB TV','BJK TV'
        ],
        'Çocuk' => [
            'COCUK','CARTOON','DISNEY','NICK','MINIKA',
            'BABY TV','BOOMERANG','CIZGI','ANIMASYON'
        ],
        'Belgesel' => [
            'BELGESEL','NATIONAL GEOGRAPHIC','NAT GEO',
            'DISCOVERY','ANIMAL PLANET','HISTORY',
            'BBC EARTH','WILD'
        ],
        'Haber' => [
            'HABER','CNN TURK','NTV','HABERTURK',
            'A HABER','TGRT HABER','BLOOMBERG HT',
            'TV 100','HALK TV'
        ],
        'Yaşam' => [
            'YASAM','TLC','DMAX','FOOD','LIFESTYLE',
            'TRAVEL','LIFE'
        ],
        'Müzik' => [
            'MUZIK','POWER','KRAL','DREAM TURK',
            'NUMBER ONE','NR1','MUSIC'
        ],
        'Sinema' => [
            'SINEMA','MOVIE','FILM','CINEMA',
            'ACTION','HBO','FILMBOX','BEIN MOVIE'
        ],
        'Yabancı' => [
            'YABANCI','INTERNATIONAL','WORLD',
            'ARABIC','RUSSIAN','ENGLISH'
        ],
        'Ulusal' => [
            'ULUSAL','ATV','KANAL D','SHOW TV',
            'STAR TV','NOW TV','FOX TV','TV8',
            'TRT 1','TRT1','KANAL 7','BEYAZ TV',
            'KANAL A'
        ]
    ];

    $categoryIds = [];
    foreach ($categories as $cat) {
        $categoryIds[normalizeLiveCategoryText((string)$cat['name'])] = (int)$cat['id'];
    }

    foreach ([$group, $name] as $source) {
        $text = normalizeLiveCategoryText($source);
        if ($text === '') continue;

        foreach ($rules as $categoryName => $keywords) {
            foreach ($keywords as $keyword) {
                $needle = normalizeLiveCategoryText($keyword);

                if ($text === $needle || str_contains($text, $needle)) {
                    $key = normalizeLiveCategoryText($categoryName);
                    if (isset($categoryIds[$key])) {
                        return $categoryIds[$key];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    $otherKey = normalizeLiveCategoryText('Diğer');
    return $categoryIds[$otherKey] ?? 0;
}

function normalizeCountryText(string $value): string {
    $value = mb_strtoupper(trim($value), 'UTF-8');
    $value = strtr($value, [
        'İ'=>'I','Ş'=>'S','Ğ'=>'G','Ü'=>'U','Ö'=>'O','Ç'=>'C'
    ]);
    return trim(preg_replace('/[^A-Z0-9]+/', ' ', $value));
}


function inferCountryIdFromChannelName(string $name, array $countries): int {
    $channel = normalizeCountryText($name);

    $rules = [
        'AL' => ['CITY NEWS ALBANIA', 'TROPOJA', 'ORA NEWS'],
        'ET' => ['ETHIOPIA'],
        'AM' => ['ARMENIA'],
        'BA' => ['BOSNIA', 'BOSNA', 'ZENICA'],
        'BH' => ['BAHRAIN'],
        'SY' => ['SYRIA', 'SOURIYA'],
        'IQ' => ['IRAQ'],
        'QA' => ['QATAR'],
        'JO' => ['JORDAN'],
        'OM' => ['OMAN'],
        'KW' => ['KTV AL', 'KTV SPORT'],
        'IR' => ['IRAN'],
        'DE' => ['DAS ERSTE', 'TAGESSCHAU24', 'PROSIEBEN', 'NDR', 'WDR', 'MDR', 'BR HD', 'HR-FERNSEHEN', 'RTL', 'SIXX'],
        'FI' => ['YLE TV1', 'YLE TEEMA', 'FINLAND'],
        'KR' => ['ARIRANG', 'SOUTH KOREA'],
        'TR' => ['TRT 1', 'TRT HABER', 'SHOW HD', 'ATV HD', 'BEYAZ TV', 'KANAL 7', 'TV8 HD', 'ULKE TV', 'HABERTURK', 'TURKIYE'],
        'UA' => ['UKRAINE'],
        'FR' => ['FRANCE 24', 'BFM'],
        'PT' => ['RTP I', 'RTP 3', 'SIC HD', 'SIC NOTICIAS'],
        'BR' => ['REDE BRASIL', 'TV CULTURA'],
        'IT' => ['RAI NEWS', 'RAI 1', 'TV YES ITALIA'],
        'ES' => ['TVE INTERNACIONAL', 'TV CUATRO'],
        'AU' => ['ABC NEWS AUSTRALIA', 'AUSBIZ'],
        'GB' => ['CNBC UK']
    ];

    foreach ($rules as $code => $keywords) {
        foreach ($keywords as $keyword) {
            if (strpos($channel, normalizeCountryText($keyword)) !== false) {
                foreach ($countries as $country) {
                    if (strtoupper((string)$country['code']) === $code) {
                        return (int)$country['id'];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

function countryIdFromValue(string $value, array $countries): int {
    $needle = normalizeCountryText($value);

    foreach ($countries as $country) {
        if ($needle !== '' && (
            $needle === normalizeCountryText((string)$country['code']) ||
            $needle === normalizeCountryText((string)$country['name'])
        )) {
            return (int)$country['id'];
        }
    }

    // Ülke bilgisi yoksa veya tanınmıyorsa Bilinmeyen Ülke.
    foreach ($countries as $country) {
        if ((string)$country['code'] === 'UNK') {
            return (int)$country['id'];
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

function countryIdFromLanguageCode(string $languageCode, array $countries): int {
    // Ülke bilgisi olmayan kanallar için varsayılan ülke eşleştirmesi.
    // Çok ülkede konuşulan diller bilerek eşleştirilmez.
    $languageCountries = [
        'ar' => 'ARB',
        'tr' => 'TR',
        'en' => 'US',
        'de' => 'DE',
        'fr' => 'FR',
        'es' => 'ES',
        'it' => 'IT',
        'ru' => 'RU',
        'nl' => 'NL',
        'el' => 'GR',
        'fi' => 'FI',
        'no' => 'NO',
        'ro' => 'RO',
        'sq' => 'AL',
        'bs' => 'BA',
        'uk' => 'UA',
        'hy' => 'AM',
        'id' => 'ID',
        'ko' => 'KR',
        'zh' => 'CN',
        'am' => 'ET'
    ];

    $languageCode = strtolower(trim($languageCode));

    if (!isset($languageCountries[$languageCode])) {
        return countryIdFromValue('', $countries);
    }

    return countryIdFromValue($languageCountries[$languageCode], $countries);
}

function normalizeLanguageCode(string $value): string {
    $value = mb_strtolower(trim($value), 'UTF-8');
    $value = strtr($value, [
        'ı'=>'i','ş'=>'s','ğ'=>'g','ü'=>'u','ö'=>'o','ç'=>'c',
        'é'=>'e','è'=>'e','ê'=>'e','á'=>'a','à'=>'a','ä'=>'a','ñ'=>'n'
    ]);
    $value = preg_replace('/[^a-z]/', '', $value);

    $names = [
        'turkish'=>'tr','turkce'=>'tr','english'=>'en',
        'german'=>'de','deutsch'=>'de','arabic'=>'ar',
        'spanish'=>'es','espanol'=>'es','french'=>'fr',
        'francais'=>'fr','italian'=>'it','russian'=>'ru',
        'portuguese'=>'pt','dutch'=>'nl','greek'=>'el',
        'persian'=>'fa','finnish'=>'fi','hindi'=>'hi',
        'armenian'=>'hy','indonesian'=>'id','korean'=>'ko',
        'norwegian'=>'no','romanian'=>'ro','ukrainian'=>'uk',
        'urdu'=>'ur','chinese'=>'zh','amharic'=>'am',
        'albanian'=>'sq','bosnian'=>'bs'
    ];

    if (isset($names[$value])) return $names[$value];

    $allowed = [
        'sq','am','ar','bs','de','el','en','es','fa','fi','fr',
        'hi','hy','id','it','ko','nl','no','pt','ro','ru','tr',
        'uk','ur','zh'
    ];

    return in_array($value, $allowed, true) ? $value : '';
}

function validStreamUrl(string $url): bool {
    if (!filter_var($url, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL) || strlen($url)>2048) return false;

    $p = parse_url($url);
    if (!$p || empty($p['scheme']) || empty($p['host'])) return false;

    if (!in_array(strtolower($p['scheme']), ['http','https'], true)) return false;

    $host = strtolower($p['host']);

    if (
        $host === 'localhost' ||
        str_ends_with($host,'.localhost') ||
        str_ends_with($host,'.local') ||
        str_ends_with($host,'.internal')
    ) return false;

    if (filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP) &&
        !filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP,FILTER_FLAG_NO_PRIV_RANGE|FILTER_FLAG_NO_RES_RANGE)
    ) return false;

    return true;
}


function inferLanguageFromChannelName(string $name): string {
    $map = [
        'AL' => 'sq',
        'AM' => 'am',
        'AR' => 'ar',
        'BS' => 'bs',
        'DE' => 'de',
        'EL' => 'el',
        'EN' => 'en',
        'ES' => 'es',
        'FA' => 'fa',
        'FI' => 'fi',
        'FR' => 'fr',
        'HI' => 'hi',
        'HY' => 'hy',
        'ID' => 'id',
        'IT' => 'it',
        'KO' => 'ko',
        'NL' => 'nl',
        'NO' => 'no',
        'PT' => 'pt',
        'RO' => 'ro',
        'RU' => 'ru',
        'TR' => 'tr',
        'UA' => 'uk',
        'UR' => 'ur',
        'ZH' => 'zh'
    ];

    if (preg_match('/^\s*([A-Z]{2})\s*[-_]/u', mb_strtoupper($name, 'UTF-8'), $m)) {
        return $map[$m[1]] ?? '';
    }

    return '';
}

function parseM3u(string $content): array {
    $content = preg_replace('/^\xEF\xBB\xBF/', '', $content);
    $lines = preg_split('/\r\n|\r|\n/', $content);
    $result = [];
    $current = null;

    foreach ($lines as $line) {
        $line = trim($line);
        if ($line === '') continue;

        if (stripos($line, '#EXTINF:') === 0) {
            $comma = strpos($line, ',');
            $meta = $comma !== false ? substr($line, 0, $comma) : $line;
            $name = $comma !== false ? trim(substr($line, $comma + 1)) : 'İsimsiz Kanal';

            $group = '';
            $logo = '';

            if (preg_match('/group-title="([^"]*)"/i', $meta, $m)) {
                $group = trim($m[1]);
            }
            if (preg_match('/tvg-logo="([^"]*)"/i', $meta, $m)) {
                $logo = trim($m[1]);
            }

            $country = '';
            $language = '';

            if (preg_match('/tvg-country="([^"]*)"/i', $meta, $m)) {
                $country = trim($m[1]);
            }

            if (preg_match('/tvg-language="([^"]*)"/i', $meta, $m)) {
                $language = trim($m[1]);
            } elseif (preg_match('/tvg-lang="([^"]*)"/i', $meta, $m)) {
                $language = trim($m[1]);
            }

            $current = [
                'name'=>mb_substr($name,0,150),
                'group'=>mb_substr($group,0,100),
                'logo'=>$logo,
                'country'=>$country,
                'language'=>$language,
                'url'=>''
            ];
            continue;
        }

        if ($line[0] === '#') continue;

        if ($current !== null && validStreamUrl($line)) {
            $current['url'] = $line;
            $result[] = $current;
            $current = null;

            if (count($result) >= 3000) break;
        }
    }

    return $result;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    try {
        $token = [GIZLENDI]
        if (!is_string($token) || !hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'], $token)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.');
        }

        $action = (string)($_POST['action'] ?? '');

        if ($action === 'load') {
            $content = '';

            if (
                isset($_FILES['m3u_file']) &&
                $_FILES['m3u_file']['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_NO_FILE
            ) {
                if ($_FILES['m3u_file']['error'] !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Dosya yüklenemedi.');
                }

                if ($_FILES['m3u_file']['size'] > 5*1024*1024) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('M3U dosyası en fazla 5 MB olabilir.');
                }

                $content = file_get_contents($_FILES['m3u_file']['tmp_name']);
                if ($content === false) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Dosya okunamadı.');
                }
            } else {
                $source = trim((string)($_POST['source_url'] ?? ''));

                if ($source === '') {
                    $source = '';
                }

                $parts = parse_url($source);

                $sourceHost = strtolower((string)($parts['host'] ?? ''));

                $allowedM3uHosts = [
                    'lol.mutlumedya.workers.dev',
                    'gist.githubusercontent.com',
                    'raw.githubusercontent.com'
                ];

                if (
                    !$parts ||
                    strtolower((string)($parts['scheme'] ?? '')) !== 'https' ||
                    !in_array($sourceHost, $allowedM3uHosts, true) ||
                    isset($parts['user']) ||
                    isset($parts['pass'])
                ) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Bu M3U adresine izin verilmiyor. HTTPS ve izin verilen kaynakları kullanın.');
                }

                if (function_exists('curl_init')) {
                    $ch = curl_init($source);
                    curl_setopt_array($ch, [
                        CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER=>true,
                        CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION=>false,
                        CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT=>10,
                        CURLOPT_TIMEOUT=>30,
                        CURLOPT_MAXFILESIZE=>5242880,
                        CURLOPT_USERAGENT=>'XtreamPanel-M3U-Importer/1.0',
                        CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER=>true,
                        CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST=>2
                    ]);
                    $content = curl_exec($ch);
                    $error = curl_error($ch);
                    $status = (int)curl_getinfo($ch,CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
                    curl_close($ch);

                    if ($content === false || $status < 200 || $status >= 300) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('M3U adresi okunamadı. HTTP: '.$status.' '.$error);
                    }
                } else {
                    $context = stream_context_create([
                        'http'=>[
                            'timeout'=>30,
                            'follow_location'=>0,
                            'user_agent'=>'XtreamPanel-M3U-Importer/1.0'
                        ],
                        'ssl'=>[
                            'verify_peer'=>true,
                            'verify_peer_name'=>true
                        ]
                    ]);
                    $content = @file_get_contents($source,false,$context);
                    if ($content === false) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('M3U adresi okunamadı. Sunucuda cURL veya HTTPS erişimini kontrol et.');
                    }
                }
            }

            if (strlen((string)$content) > 5*1024*1024) {
                throw new RuntimeException('M3U içeriği 5 MB sınırını aşıyor.');
            }

            if (stripos((string)$content,'#EXTM3U') === false) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçerli bir M3U listesi bulunamadı.');
            }

            $items = parseM3u((string)$content);

            if (!$items) {
                throw new RuntimeException('M3U içinde aktarılabilir kanal bulunamadı.');
            }

            foreach ($items as &$item) {
                $countryValue = trim((string)($item['country'] ?? ''));

                $explicitLanguage = normalizeLanguageCode(
                    (string)($item['language'] ?? '')
                );

                $item['language_code'] = $explicitLanguage !== ''
                    ? $explicitLanguage
                    : inferLanguageFromChannelName((string)($item['name'] ?? ''));

                // M3U'da açık ülke bilgisi varsa onu koru.
                // Ülke bilgisi yoksa dil koduna göre varsayılan ülke seç.
                if ($countryValue !== '') {
                    $item['country_id'] = countryIdFromValue(
                        $countryValue,
                        $countries
                    );
                } else {
                    $item['country_id'] = countryIdFromLanguageCode(
                        (string)$item['language_code'],
                        $countries
                    );
                }
            }
            unset($item);

            $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] = $items;
            $message = count($items).' kanal bulundu. Aktarmak istediklerini seç.';
        }

        if ($action === 'import') {
            $items = $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] ?? [];

            $selected = $_POST['selected'] ?? [];
            $packageIds = $_POST['package_ids'] ?? [];

            if (!is_array($selected) || !is_array($packageIds)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Seçimler geçersiz.');
            }

            $selected = array_values(array_unique(array_map('intval',$selected)));
            $packageIds = array_values(array_unique(array_map('intval',$packageIds)));

            if (!$selected) {
                throw new RuntimeException('En az bir kanal seçmelisin.');
            }

            if (!$packageIds) {
                throw new RuntimeException('En az bir paket seçmelisin.');
            }

            $marks = implode(',',array_fill(0,count($packageIds),'?'));
            $checkPackages = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM packages WHERE status='active' AND id IN ($marks)"
            );
            $checkPackages->execute($packageIds);
            $validPackages = array_map('intval',$checkPackages->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));

            if (count($validPackages) !== count($packageIds)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Paketlerden biri geçersiz veya pasif.');
            }

            $findGlobalCategory = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM categories
                 WHERE type='live' AND country_id IS NULL
                 AND LOWER(name)=LOWER(?) LIMIT 1"
            );

            $findCountryCategory = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM categories
                 WHERE type='live' AND country_id=?
                 AND LOWER(name)=LOWER(?) LIMIT 1"
            );

            $addCountryCategory = $pdo->prepare(
                "INSERT INTO categories (name,type,sort_order,country_id)
                 VALUES (?,'live',?,?)"
            );

            $duplicateCheck = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM streams WHERE type='live' AND stream_url=? LIMIT 1"
            );

            $logoUpdate = $pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE streams SET logo_url=? WHERE id=? AND type='live'
                 AND (logo_url IS NULL OR logo_url='')"
            );

            $insert = $pdo->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO streams
                (category_id,name,stream_url,type,status,tmdb_id,poster_path,backdrop_path,
