            );

            $added = 0;
            $duplicates = 0;
            $invalid = 0;

            $pdo->beginTransaction();

            try {
                foreach ($selected as $index) {
                    if (!isset($items[$index])) {
                        $invalid++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    $item = $items[$index];
                    $name = trim((string)$item['name']);
                    $url = trim((string)$item['url']);

                    if ($name === '' || !validStreamUrl($url)) {
                        $invalid++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    $countryId = (int)($_POST['country'][$index] ?? ($item['country_id'] ?? 0));

                    if ($countryId > 0 && !isset($countryMap[$countryId])) {
                        $invalid++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    $categoryName = trim((string)($_POST['category'][$index] ?? 'Diğer'));

                    $allowedCategory = false;
                    foreach ($categoryDefinitions as $definedCategory) {
                        if (normalizeLiveCategoryText($definedCategory) ===
                            normalizeLiveCategoryText($categoryName)) {
                            $categoryName = $definedCategory;
                            $allowedCategory = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    if (!$allowedCategory) {
                        $categoryName = 'Diğer';
                    }

                    $categoryId = 0;

                    if ($countryId > 0) {
                        $findCountryCategory->execute([$countryId, $categoryName]);
                        $categoryId = (int)$findCountryCategory->fetchColumn();

                        if ($categoryId < 1) {
                            $sortOrder = 10;

                            foreach ($categories as $globalCategory) {
                                if (normalizeLiveCategoryText((string)$globalCategory['name']) ===
                                    normalizeLiveCategoryText($categoryName)) {
                                    $sortOrder = (int)$globalCategory['sort_order'];
                                    break;
                                }
                            }

                            $addCountryCategory->execute([
                                $categoryName,
                                $sortOrder,
                                $countryId
                            ]);

                            $categoryId = (int)$pdo->lastInsertId();
                        }
                    } else {
                        $findGlobalCategory->execute([$categoryName]);
                        $categoryId = (int)$findGlobalCategory->fetchColumn();
                    }

                    $postedLanguage = trim((string)($_POST['language'][$index] ?? ''));

                    if ($postedLanguage === '__unknown__') {
                        $languageCode = '';
                    } elseif ($postedLanguage !== '') {
                        $languageCode = normalizeLanguageCode($postedLanguage);
                    } else {
                        $languageCode = normalizeLanguageCode((string)($item['language_code'] ?? ''));
                    }

                    $adult = isset($_POST['adult'][$index]) ? 1 : 0;

                    $logo = trim((string)($item['logo'] ?? ''));

                    if ($logo !== '' && !validStreamUrl($logo)) {
                        $logo = '';
                    }

                    $duplicateCheck->execute([$url]);
                    $existingId = $duplicateCheck->fetchColumn();

                    if ($existingId) {
                        if ($logo !== '') {
                            $logoUpdate->execute([$logo, (int)$existingId]);
                        }

                        $duplicateCountryLanguageUpdate = $pdo->prepare(
                            "UPDATE streams
                             SET country_id = CASE
                                 WHEN country_id IS NULL OR country_id = 0
                                 THEN ? ELSE country_id END,
                                 language_code = CASE
                                 WHEN language_code IS NULL OR language_code = ''
                                 THEN ? ELSE language_code END
                             WHERE id = ? AND type = 'live'"
                        );

                        $duplicateCountryLanguageUpdate->execute([
                            $countryId > 0 ? $countryId : null,
                            $languageCode !== '' ? $languageCode : null,
                            (int)$existingId
                        ]);

                        $duplicates++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    $insert->execute([
                        $categoryId > 0 ? $categoryId : null,
                        mb_substr($name,0,150),
                        $url,
                        $logo !== '' ? $logo : null,
                        $countryId > 0 ? $countryId : null,
                        $languageCode !== '' ? $languageCode : null
                    ]);

                    $streamId = (int)$pdo->lastInsertId();

                    $adultUpdate->execute([$adult,$streamId]);

                    foreach ($validPackages as $packageId) {
                        $packageInsert->execute([$streamId,$packageId]);
                    }

                    $added++;
                }

                $pdo->commit();
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) $pdo->rollBack();
                throw $e;
            }

            $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] = [];

            $message = "Aktarım tamamlandı. Eklenen: $added | Zaten kayıtlı: $duplicates | Atlanan: $invalid";
            $_SESSION['m3u_import_result'] = $message;
            header('Location: /m3u_import.php?done=1');
            exit;
        }

        if ($action === 'clear') {
            $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] = [];
            $items = [];
            $message = 'M3U kanal listesi temizlendi.';
        }

    } catch (Throwable $e) {
        $message = $e->getMessage();
        $messageType = 'error';
        $items = $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] ?? [];
    }
}

if (isset($_GET['done']) && !empty($_SESSION['m3u_import_result'])) {
    $message = $_SESSION['m3u_import_result'];
    unset($_SESSION['m3u_import_result']);
}

$items = $_SESSION['m3u_import_items'] ?? [];

$suggestedCategoryName = 'Diğer';

if ($suggestedCategoryId === 0) {

========== DOSYA SONU: m3u_import.php ==========

========== DOSYA: series.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors','0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime'=>0,
    'path'=>'/',
    'secure'=>true,
    'httponly'=>true,
    'samesite'=>'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function e($v): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$v,ENT_QUOTES,'UTF-8');
}
function redirectTo(string $url='series.php'): never {
    header('Location: '.$url);
    exit;
}
function validStreamUrl(string $url): bool {
    if (!filter_var($url,FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)) return false;
    $p=parse_url($url);
    if (!$p || !in_array(strtolower($p['scheme']??''),['http','https'],true)) return false;
    $host=strtolower(trim($p['host']??''));
    if ($host==='' || $host==='localhost' || str_ends_with($host,'.local')) return false;
    if (filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP)) {
        $flags=FILTER_FLAG_NO_PRIV_RANGE|FILTER_FLAG_NO_RES_RANGE;
        if (!filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP,$flags)) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    redirectTo('/');
}
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['csrf']=bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}

$config=parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');
$pdo=new PDO(
    "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
    $config['DB_USER'],
    $config['DB_PASS'],
    [
        PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE=>PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
        PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE=>PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
        PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES=>false
    ]
);

$error='';
$success='';


function seriesTmdbApi(string $path): array {
    $cfg = parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/tmdb.ini');
    $key = is_array($cfg) ? trim((string)($cfg['TMDB_API_KEY'] ?? '')) : '';
    if ($key === '') {
        throw new RuntimeException('TMDB API anahtarı sunucuda bulunamadı.');
    }

    $url = 'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/' . ltrim($path, '/');
    $url .= (str_contains($url, '?') ? '&' : '?') .
            'api_key= [GIZLENDI]

    $ch = curl_init($url);
    curl_setopt_array($ch, [
        CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
        CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => 10,
        CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,
        CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION => false,
        CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => ['Accept: application/json']
    ]);
    $body = curl_exec($ch);
    $http = (int)curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
    $err = curl_error($ch);
    curl_close($ch);

    if ($body === false || $err !== '') {
        throw new RuntimeException('TMDB bağlantı hatası: ' . $err);
    }
    $data = json_decode($body, true);
    if ($http !== 200 || !is_array($data)) {
        throw new RuntimeException('TMDB isteği başarısız oldu (HTTP ' . $http . ').');
    }
    return $data;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD']==='POST') {
    try {
        $token= [GIZLENDI]
        if (!is_string($token)||!hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'],$token)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.');
        }

        $action=$_POST['action']??'';

        if ($action === 'import_tmdb_series') {
            header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
            set_time_limit(240);

            try {
                $tmdbId = (int)($_POST['tmdb_id'] ?? 0);
                if ($tmdbId < 1) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Geçerli bir TMDB dizi numarası gönderilmedi.');
                }

                // Önce TMDB verilerinin tamamını al. Veritabanı işlemi bundan sonra başlar.
                $detail = seriesTmdbApi('tv/' . $tmdbId);
                $seasonList = $detail['seasons'] ?? [];
                $prepared = [];
                $totalEpisodes = 0;

                foreach ($seasonList as $seasonInfo) {
                    $seasonNo = (int)($seasonInfo['season_number'] ?? -1);
                    if ($seasonNo < 0) continue;

                    $seasonData = seriesTmdbApi(
                        'tv/' . $tmdbId . '/season/' . $seasonNo
                    );
                    $episodesData = $seasonData['episodes'] ?? [];
                    $prepared[] = [
                        'info' => $seasonInfo,
                        'data' => $seasonData,
                        'episodes' => $episodesData
                    ];
                    $totalEpisodes += count($episodesData);
                }

                $categoryId = (int)($_POST['category_id'] ?? 0);
                if ($categoryId > 0) {
                    $cat = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
                    $cat->execute([$categoryId]);
                    if (!$cat->fetchColumn()) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen dizi kategorisi geçerli değil.');
                    }
                }

                $pdo->beginTransaction();

                $findSeries = $pdo->prepare(
                    "SELECT * FROM streams WHERE type='series' AND tmdb_id=? LIMIT 1"
                );
                $findSeries->execute([$tmdbId]);
                $existingSeries = $findSeries->fetch();

                $title = trim((string)($detail['name'] ?? $detail['original_name'] ?? 'İsimsiz dizi'));
                $originalName = (string)($detail['original_name'] ?? '');
                $overview = (string)($detail['overview'] ?? '');
                $poster = (string)($detail['poster_path'] ?? '');
                $backdrop = (string)($detail['backdrop_path'] ?? '');
                $releaseDate = (string)($detail['first_air_date'] ?? '');
                $releaseYear = $releaseDate !== '' ? substr($releaseDate, 0, 4) : '';
                $vote = (float)($detail['vote_average'] ?? 0);
                $genres = implode(', ', array_map(
                    static fn($g) => (string)($g['name'] ?? ''),
                    $detail['genres'] ?? []
                ));

                if ($existingSeries) {
                    $seriesId = (int)$existingSeries['id'];
                    $finalCategory = $categoryId > 0
                        ? $categoryId
                        : ($existingSeries['category_id'] ?? null);

                    $up = $pdo->prepare("
                        UPDATE streams SET
                            category_id=?, name=?, original_name=?, overview=?,
                            poster_path=?, backdrop_path=?, release_date=?,
                            release_year=?, vote_average=?, genres=?
                        WHERE id=? AND type='series'
                    ");
                    $up->execute([
                        $finalCategory, $title, $originalName, $overview,
                        $poster, $backdrop, $releaseDate, $releaseYear,
                        $vote, $genres, $seriesId
                    ]);
                } else {
                    $ins = $pdo->prepare("
                        INSERT INTO streams
                        (category_id,name,stream_url,type,status,tmdb_id,
                         poster_path,backdrop_path,overview,release_date,
                         release_year,vote_average,genres,original_name)
                        VALUES (?,?,NULL,'series','active',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
                    ");
                    $ins->execute([
                        $categoryId > 0 ? $categoryId : null,
                        $title, $tmdbId, $poster, $backdrop, $overview,
                        $releaseDate, $releaseYear, $vote, $genres, $originalName
                    ]);
                    $seriesId = (int)$pdo->lastInsertId();
                }

                $seasonCreated = 0;
                $seasonUpdated = 0;
                $episodeCreated = 0;
                $episodeUpdated = 0;

                $findSeason = $pdo->prepare(
                    "SELECT id FROM seasons WHERE series_id=? AND season_number=? LIMIT 1"
                );
                $insertSeason = $pdo->prepare("
                    INSERT INTO seasons
                    (series_id,season_number,name,tmdb_id,overview,poster_path,air_date,vote_average)
                    VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
                ");
                $updateSeason = $pdo->prepare("
                    UPDATE seasons SET name=?,tmdb_id=?,overview=?,poster_path=?,air_date=?,vote_average=?
                    WHERE id=?
                ");

                $findEpisode = $pdo->prepare(
                    "SELECT id FROM episodes WHERE season_id=? AND episode_number=? LIMIT 1"
                );
                $insertEpisode = $pdo->prepare("
                    INSERT INTO episodes
                    (season_id,episode_number,name,stream_url,duration,status,tmdb_id,overview,still_path,air_date,vote_average)
                    VALUES (?,?,?,'',?,'active',?,?,?,?,?)
                ");
                $updateEpisode = $pdo->prepare("
                    UPDATE episodes SET name=?,tmdb_id=?,overview=?,still_path=?,air_date=?,vote_average=?,
                    duration=COALESCE(duration,?)
                    WHERE id=?
                ");

                foreach ($prepared as $bundle) {
                    $si = $bundle['info'];
                    $sd = $bundle['data'];
                    $seasonNo = (int)($si['season_number'] ?? 0);
                    $seasonName = trim((string)($sd['name'] ?? $si['name'] ?? 'Sezon ' . $seasonNo));
                    $seasonTmdb = (int)($sd['id'] ?? $si['id'] ?? 0);
                    $seasonOverview = (string)($sd['overview'] ?? '');
                    $seasonPoster = (string)($sd['poster_path'] ?? '');
                    $seasonAir = (string)($sd['air_date'] ?? '');
                    $seasonVote = (float)($sd['vote_average'] ?? 0);

                    $findSeason->execute([$seriesId, $seasonNo]);
                    $seasonId = (int)($findSeason->fetchColumn() ?: 0);

                    if ($seasonId) {
                        $updateSeason->execute([
                            $seasonName, $seasonTmdb ?: null, $seasonOverview,
                            $seasonPoster, $seasonAir, $seasonVote, $seasonId
                        ]);
                        $seasonUpdated++;
                    } else {
                        $insertSeason->execute([
                            $seriesId, $seasonNo, $seasonName,
                            $seasonTmdb ?: null, $seasonOverview,
                            $seasonPoster, $seasonAir, $seasonVote
                        ]);
                        $seasonId = (int)$pdo->lastInsertId();
                        $seasonCreated++;
                    }

                    foreach ($bundle['episodes'] as $ep) {
                        $episodeNo = (int)($ep['episode_number'] ?? 0);
                        if ($episodeNo < 1) continue;

                        $episodeName = trim((string)($ep['name'] ?? 'Bölüm ' . $episodeNo));
                        $episodeTmdb = (int)($ep['id'] ?? 0);
                        $episodeOverview = (string)($ep['overview'] ?? '');
                        $still = (string)($ep['still_path'] ?? '');
                        $air = (string)($ep['air_date'] ?? '');
                        $episodeVote = (float)($ep['vote_average'] ?? 0);
                        $runtime = isset($ep['runtime']) && (int)$ep['runtime'] > 0
                            ? (int)$ep['runtime'] : null;

                        $findEpisode->execute([$seasonId, $episodeNo]);
                        $episodeId = (int)($findEpisode->fetchColumn() ?: 0);

                        if ($episodeId) {
                            // Var olan yayın adresine ve aktif/pasif durumuna dokunma.
                            $updateEpisode->execute([
                                $episodeName, $episodeTmdb ?: null, $episodeOverview,
                                $still, $air, $episodeVote, $runtime, $episodeId
                            ]);
                            $episodeUpdated++;
                        } else {
                            $insertEpisode->execute([
                                $seasonId, $episodeNo, $episodeName, $runtime,
                                $episodeTmdb ?: null, $episodeOverview,
                                $still, $air, $episodeVote
                            ]);
                            $episodeCreated++;
                        }
                    }
                }

                $pdo->commit();

                echo json_encode([
                    'success' => true,
                    'series_id' => $seriesId,
                    'seasons_created' => $seasonCreated,
                    'seasons_updated' => $seasonUpdated,
                    'episodes_created' => $episodeCreated,
                    'episodes_updated' => $episodeUpdated,
                    'seasons_total' => count($prepared),
                    'episodes_total' => $totalEpisodes
                ], JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
                exit;

            } catch (Throwable $ex) {
                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) $pdo->rollBack();
                error_log('TMDB series import error: ' . $ex->getMessage());
                http_response_code(400);
                echo json_encode([
                    'success' => false,
                    'error' => $ex->getMessage()
                ], JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
                exit;
            }
        }


        if ($action==='save_series') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $name=trim($_POST['name']??'');
            $category=(int)($_POST['category_id']??0);

            if ($name===''||mb_strlen($name)>200) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Dizi adı boş olamaz ve 200 karakteri geçemez.');
            }

            if ($category>0) {
                $check=$pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
                $check->execute([$category]);
                if (!$check->fetch()) throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen dizi kategorisi geçersiz.');
            } else {
                $category=null;
            }

            $tmdbId=(int)($_POST['tmdb_id']??0);
            $poster=trim((string)($_POST['poster_path']??''));
            $backdrop=trim((string)($_POST['backdrop_path']??''));
            $overview=trim((string)($_POST['overview']??''));
            $releaseDate=trim((string)($_POST['release_date']??''));
            $releaseYear=(int)($_POST['release_year']??0);
            $vote=(float)($_POST['vote_average']??0);
            $genres=trim((string)($_POST['genres']??''));
            $original=trim((string)($_POST['original_name']??''));
            $logo=trim((string)($_POST['logo_url']??''));

            if ($tmdbId<0||$releaseYear<0||$releaseYear>2200||
                !is_finite($vote)||$vote<0||$vote>10) {
                throw new RuntimeException('TMDB dizi bilgileri geçersiz.');
            }
            if (strlen($poster)>500||strlen($backdrop)>500||
                strlen($releaseDate)>20||strlen($overview)>10000||
                strlen($genres)>10000||strlen($original)>255||strlen($logo)>1000) {
                throw new RuntimeException('TMDB dizi bilgileri izin verilen uzunluğu aşıyor.');
            }
            if ($logo!==''&&(!filter_var($logo,FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)||
                !in_array(strtolower((string)parse_url($logo,PHP_URL_SCHEME)),['http','https'],true))) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Logo adresi HTTP veya HTTPS olmalı.');
            }

            $metadata=[$name,$category,$tmdbId?:null,$poster?:null,$backdrop?:null,
                $overview?:null,$releaseDate?:null,$releaseYear?:null,$vote,
                $genres?:null,$original?:null,$logo?:null];

            if ($id>0) {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("UPDATE streams SET name=?,category_id=?,tmdb_id=?,
                    poster_path=?,backdrop_path=?,overview=?,release_date=?,release_year=?,
                    vote_average=?,genres=?,original_name=?,logo_url=?
                    WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
                $stmt->execute([...$metadata,$id]);
                if (!$stmt->rowCount()) {
                    $check=$pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
                    $check->execute([$id]);
                    if (!$check->fetch()) throw new RuntimeException('Dizi kaydı bulunamadı.');
                }
            } else {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO streams
                    (name,category_id,stream_url,type,status,tmdb_id,poster_path,
                    backdrop_path,overview,release_date,release_year,vote_average,
                    genres,original_name,logo_url)
                    VALUES (?,?,NULL,'series','active',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
                $stmt->execute([$name,$category,$tmdbId?:null,$poster?:null,
                    $backdrop?:null,$overview?:null,$releaseDate?:null,
                    $releaseYear?:null,$vote,$genres?:null,$original?:null,$logo?:null]);
            }
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='save_season') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $series=(int)($_POST['series_id']??0);
            $number=(int)($_POST['season_number']??0);
            $name=trim($_POST['name']??'');

            $check=$pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
            $check->execute([$series]);
            if (!$check->fetch()) throw new RuntimeException('Dizi bulunamadı.');
            if ($number<1||$number>999) throw new RuntimeException('Sezon numarası 1-999 arasında olmalı.');
            if ($name===''||mb_strlen($name)>190) throw new RuntimeException('Sezon adı geçersiz.');

            if ($id>0) {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("UPDATE seasons SET series_id=?,season_number=?,name=? WHERE id=?");
                $stmt->execute([$series,$number,$name,$id]);
            } else {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO seasons (series_id,season_number,name) VALUES (?,?,?)");
                $stmt->execute([$series,$number,$name]);
            }
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='save_episode') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $season=(int)($_POST['season_id']??0);
            $number=(int)($_POST['episode_number']??0);
            $name=trim($_POST['name']??'');
            $url=trim($_POST['stream_url']??'');
            $duration=trim($_POST['duration']??'');

            $check=$pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM seasons WHERE id=?");
            $check->execute([$season]);
            if (!$check->fetch()) throw new RuntimeException('Sezon bulunamadı.');
            if ($number<1||$number>99999) throw new RuntimeException('Bölüm numarası geçersiz.');
            if ($name===''||mb_strlen($name)>190) throw new RuntimeException('Bölüm adı geçersiz.');
            if (!validStreamUrl($url)) throw new RuntimeException('Geçerli bir HTTP/HTTPS yayın adresi gir.');
            $minutes=$duration===''?null:(int)$duration;
            if ($minutes!==null&&($minutes<1||$minutes>10000)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Süre 1-10000 dakika arasında olmalı.');
            }

            if ($id>0) {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("UPDATE episodes SET season_id=?,episode_number=?,name=?,stream_url=?,duration=? WHERE id=?");
                $stmt->execute([$season,$number,$name,$url,$minutes,$id]);
            } else {
                $stmt=$pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO episodes (season_id,episode_number,name,stream_url,duration) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)");
                $stmt->execute([$season,$number,$name,$url,$minutes]);
            }
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='toggle_episode') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $stmt=$pdo->prepare("UPDATE episodes SET status=IF(status='active','disabled','active') WHERE id=?");
            $stmt->execute([$id]);
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='delete_episode') {
            $stmt=$pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM episodes WHERE id=?");
            $stmt->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0)]);
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='delete_season') {
            $stmt=$pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM seasons WHERE id=?");
            $stmt->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0)]);
            redirectTo();
        }

        if ($action==='delete_series') {
            $stmt=$pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
            $stmt->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0)]);
            redirectTo();
        }

    } catch (PDOException $ex) {
        if ((string)$ex->getCode()==='23000') {
            $error='Bu sezon veya bölüm numarası zaten kullanılıyor.';
        } else {
            error_log('Series panel DB error: '.$ex->getMessage());
            $error='Veritabanı işlemi tamamlanamadı.';
        }
    } catch (Throwable $ex) {
        $error=$ex->getMessage();
    }
}

$seriesList=$pdo->query("
    SELECT s.id,s.name,s.category_id,c.name AS category_name,
           (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM seasons se WHERE se.series_id=s.id) AS season_count,
           (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM episodes ep JOIN seasons se2 ON se2.id=ep.season_id WHERE se2.series_id=s.id) AS episode_count
    FROM streams s
    LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id=s.category_id
    WHERE s.type='series'
    ORDER BY s.name
")->fetchAll();

$categories=$pdo->query("SELECT id,name FROM categories WHERE type='series' ORDER BY sort_order,name")->fetchAll();

$seasons=$pdo->query("
    SELECT se.*,s.name AS series_name
    FROM seasons se
    JOIN streams s ON s.id=se.series_id
    ORDER BY s.name,se.season_number
")->fetchAll();

$episodes=$pdo->query("
    SELECT ep.*,se.name AS season_name,se.season_number,
           s.name AS series_name
    FROM episodes ep
    JOIN seasons se ON se.id=ep.season_id
    JOIN streams s ON s.id=se.series_id
    ORDER BY s.name,se.season_number,ep.episode_number
")->fetchAll();

$editSeries=null;
$editSeason=null;
$editEpisode=null;

if (isset($_GET['edit_series'])) {
    $st=$pdo->prepare("SELECT * FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type='series'");
    $st->execute([(int)$_GET['edit_series']]);
    $editSeries=$st->fetch()?:null;
}
if (isset($_GET['edit_season'])) {
    $st=$pdo->prepare("SELECT * FROM seasons WHERE id=?");
    $st->execute([(int)$_GET['edit_season']]);
    $editSeason=$st->fetch()?:null;
}
if (isset($_GET['edit_episode'])) {
    $st=$pdo->prepare("SELECT * FROM episodes WHERE id=?");
    $st->execute([(int)$_GET['edit_episode']]);
    $editEpisode=$st->fetch()?:null;
}

$seasonOptions=$pdo->query("
    SELECT se.id,se.name,se.season_number,s.name AS series_name
    FROM seasons se JOIN streams s ON s.id=se.series_id
    ORDER BY s.name,se.season_number
")->fetchAll();

<script>
function openMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('overlay').classList.add('show');
    document.body.classList.add('menu-open');
}
function closeMenu(){
    document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('show');
    document.body.classList.remove('menu-open');
}
document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape')closeMenu();});

function seriesImg(path,size='w500'){
    return path?'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/'+size+path:'';
}
function seriesMsg(message){
    document.getElementById('seriesTmdbMessage').textContent=message;
}
async function searchSeriesTMDB(){
    const q=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbQuery').value.trim();
    const btn=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbSearchBtn');
    const box=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbResults');
    if(q.length<2){seriesMsg('En az 2 karakter yaz.');return;}
    btn.disabled=true;box.replaceChildren();seriesMsg('TMDB dizileri aranıyor...');
    try{
        const r=await fetch('tmdb.php?action=search&kind=tv&q='+encodeURIComponent(q),{
            credentials:'same-origin',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}
        });
        const d=await r.json();
        if(!r.ok||!d.success)throw Error(d.error||'Dizi araması başarısız.');
        if(!d.results||!d.results.length){seriesMsg('Sonuç bulunamadı.');return;}
        seriesMsg(d.results.length+' sonuç bulundu. Diziyi seç.');
        d.results.forEach(item=>{
            const b=document.createElement('button');
            b.type='button';b.className='tmdb-result';
            const image=document.createElement('img');
            image.src=seriesImg(item.poster_path,'w185');
            image.alt='';image.loading='lazy';
            const title=document.createElement('strong');
            title.textContent=item.title||'İsimsiz dizi';
            const year=document.createElement('span');
            year.textContent=(item.release_date||'').slice(0,4);
            b.append(image,title,year);
            b.addEventListener('click',()=>selectSeriesTMDB(item.id));
            box.append(b);
        });
    }catch(e){seriesMsg(e.message||'TMDB bağlantı hatası.');}
    finally{btn.disabled=false;}
}
async function selectSeriesTMDB(id){
    const btn=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbSearchBtn');
    btn.disabled=true;
    seriesMsg('Dizi bilgileri ve bütün sezonlar/bölümler TMDB üzerinden alınıyor. Lütfen bekle...');
    try{
        const csrf=document.querySelector('input[name="csrf"]')?.value||'';
        const category=document.querySelector('select[name="category_id"]');
        const body=new URLSearchParams({
            action:'import_tmdb_series',
            csrf:csrf,
            tmdb_id:String(id),
            category_id:category?category.value:''
        });

        const r=await fetch('series.php',{
            method:'POST',
            credentials:'same-origin',
            headers:{
                'Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8',
                'Accept':'application/json'
            },
            body:body.toString()
        });
        const d=await r.json();
        if(!r.ok||!d.success) throw Error(d.error||'Dizi ve bölümler içe aktarılamadı.');

        seriesMsg(
            'Aktarım tamamlandı! Sezon: '+d.seasons_total+
            ' · Yeni sezon: '+d.seasons_created+
            ' · Bölüm: '+d.episodes_total+
            ' · Yeni bölüm: '+d.episodes_created+
            ' · Güncellenen bölüm: '+d.episodes_updated+
            '. Sayfa yenileniyor...'
        );
        alert(
            'Dizi başarıyla aktarıldı.\\n\\n'+
            'Sezon: '+d.seasons_total+' (Yeni: '+d.seasons_created+')\\n'+
            'Bölüm: '+d.episodes_total+' (Yeni: '+d.episodes_created+')\\n'+
            'Güncellenen bölüm: '+d.episodes_updated+'\\n\\n'+
            'Bölüm yayın adreslerini artık tek tek girebilirsin.'
        );
        window.location.href='series.php';
    }catch(e){
        seriesMsg('HATA: '+(e.message||'TMDB aktarımı başarısız.'));
        alert('TMDB aktarım hatası: '+(e.message||'Bilinmeyen hata'));
    }finally{
        btn.disabled=false;
    }
}
document.getElementById('seriesTmdbQuery').addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();searchSeriesTMDB();}
});
(function(){
    const id=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbId').value;
    if(!id)return;
    const posterPath=document.getElementById('seriesPosterPath').value;
    document.getElementById('seriesTmdbTitle').textContent=document.querySelector('input[name="name"]').value;
    document.getElementById('seriesTmdbInfo').textContent=
        'TMDB ID: '+id+' · Yıl: '+document.getElementById('seriesReleaseYear').value+
        ' · Puan: '+document.getElementById('seriesVoteAverage').value+'/10';
    document.getElementById('seriesTmdbOverview').textContent=
        document.getElementById('seriesOverview').value;
    const poster=document.getElementById('seriesTmdbPoster');
    poster.src=seriesImg(posterPath);poster.style.display=posterPath?'block':'none';
    document.getElementById('seriesTmdbSelected').style.display='flex';
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

========== DOSYA SONU: series.php ==========

========== DOSYA: _content.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors','0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

$type = $CONTENT_TYPE ?? '';
if (!in_array($type,['live','movie'],true)) {
    http_response_code(404);
    exit('Sayfa bulunamadı.');
}

$page = $type === 'live' ? 'live.php' : 'movies.php';
$pageTitle = $type === 'live' ? 'Canlı TV Yönetimi' : 'Film Yönetimi';
$itemLabel = $type === 'live' ? 'Kanal' : 'Film';

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime'=>0,'path'=>'/','secure'=>true,'httponly'=>true,'samesite'=>'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
header('X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN');
header('Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin');

function ce($v): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string)$v,ENT_QUOTES,'UTF-8');
}

$languageNames = [
    'sq'=>'Arnavutça','am'=>'Amharca','ar'=>'Arapça','bs'=>'Boşnakça',
    'de'=>'Almanca','el'=>'Yunanca','en'=>'İngilizce','es'=>'İspanyolca',
    'fa'=>'Farsça','fi'=>'Fince','fr'=>'Fransızca','hi'=>'Hintçe',
    'hy'=>'Ermenice','id'=>'Endonezce','it'=>'İtalyanca','ko'=>'Korece',
    'nl'=>'Felemenkçe','no'=>'Norveççe','pt'=>'Portekizce','ro'=>'Romence',
    'ru'=>'Rusça','tr'=>'Türkçe','uk'=>'Ukraynaca','ur'=>'Urduca','zh'=>'Çince'
];
function contentGo(string $url): never {
    header('Location: '.$url);
    exit;
}
if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) contentGo('/');
if (empty($_SESSION['csrf'])) $_SESSION['csrf']=bin2hex(random_bytes(32));

try {
    $config=parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/database.ini');
    if (!$config) throw new RuntimeException();
    $pdo=new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$config['DB_HOST']};dbname={$config['DB_NAME']};charset=utf8mb4",
        $config['DB_USER'],$config['DB_PASS'],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE=>PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
            PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE=>PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,
            PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES=>false
        ]
    );
} catch(Throwable $ex) {
    http_response_code(500);
    exit('Veritabanı bağlantısı kurulamadı.');
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD']==='POST') {
    try {
        $token= [GIZLENDI]
        if (!is_string($token)||!hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf'],$token)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Güvenlik doğrulaması başarısız.');
        }

        $action=(string)($_POST['action']??'');

        if ($action==='logout') {
            $_SESSION=[];
            session_destroy();
            contentGo('/');
        }

        if ($action==='category_save') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $name=trim((string)($_POST['name']??''));
            $sort=(int)($_POST['sort_order']??0);

            if ($name===''||mb_strlen($name)>100) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Kategori adı 1-100 karakter olmalı.');
            }

            if ($id>0) {
                $st=$pdo->prepare('UPDATE categories SET name=?,sort_order=? WHERE id=? AND type=?');
                $st->execute([$name,$sort,$id,$type]);
                $_SESSION['flash']=['success','Kategori güncellendi.'];
            } else {
                $st=$pdo->prepare('INSERT INTO categories (name,type,sort_order) VALUES (?,?,?)');
                $st->execute([$name,$type,$sort]);
                $_SESSION['flash']=['success','Kategori oluşturuldu.'];
            }
            contentGo($page.'#categories');
        }

        if ($action==='category_delete') {
            $st=$pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM categories WHERE id=? AND type=?');
            $st->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0),$type]);
            $_SESSION['flash']=['success','Kategori silme işlemi tamamlandı.'];
            contentGo($page.'#categories');
        }

        if ($action==='stream_save') {
            $id=(int)($_POST['id']??0);
            $name=trim((string)($_POST['name']??''));
            $url=trim((string)($_POST['stream_url']??''));
            $category=(int)($_POST['category_id']??0);
            $status=(string)($_POST['status']??'active');

            if ($name===''||mb_strlen($name)>150) {
                throw new RuntimeException('İsim 1-150 karakter olmalı.');
            }
            if (!in_array($status,['active','disabled'],true)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Yayın durumu geçersiz.');
            }
            if ($url===''||strlen($url)>2048||!filter_var($url,FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Geçerli bir HTTP/HTTPS yayın adresi girin.');
            }
            $parts=parse_url($url);
            if (!$parts||!isset($parts['scheme'],$parts['host'])||
                !in_array(strtolower($parts['scheme']),['http','https'],true)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Yayın adresi HTTP veya HTTPS olmalı.');
            }
            $host=strtolower($parts['host']);
            if ($host==='localhost'||str_ends_with($host,'.localhost')||
                str_ends_with($host,'.local')||str_ends_with($host,'.internal')) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Yerel ağ adresleri kabul edilmiyor.');
            }
            if (filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP) &&
                !filter_var($host,FILTER_VALIDATE_IP,FILTER_FLAG_NO_PRIV_RANGE|FILTER_FLAG_NO_RES_RANGE)) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Özel veya yerel IP adresleri kabul edilmiyor.');
            }

            if ($category>0) {
                $st=$pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id=? AND type=?');
                $st->execute([$category,$type]);
                if (!$st->fetch()) throw new RuntimeException('Kategori bu içerik türüne ait değil.');
            } else {
                $category=null;
            }

            $selectedPackageIds=[];
            $isAdult=0;
            $languageCode='';

            if ($type==='live') {
                $rawPackageIds=$_POST['package_ids']??[];
                if (!is_array($rawPackageIds)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Paket seçimi geçersiz.');
                }

                foreach ($rawPackageIds as $packageId) {
                    $validId=filter_var($packageId,FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
                    if ($validId===false || $validId<1) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Paket seçimi geçersiz.');
                    }
                    $selectedPackageIds[]=(int)$validId;
                }

                $selectedPackageIds=array_values(array_unique($selectedPackageIds));

                if (!$selectedPackageIds) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('En az bir paket seçmelisin.');
                }

                $marks=implode(',',array_fill(0,count($selectedPackageIds),'?'));
                $checkPackages=$pdo->prepare(
                    "SELECT id FROM packages WHERE status='active' AND id IN ($marks)"
                );
                $checkPackages->execute($selectedPackageIds);
                $validPackages=array_map('intval',$checkPackages->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));

                if (count($validPackages)!==count($selectedPackageIds)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Seçilen paketlerden biri aktif değil veya bulunamadı.');
                }

                $adultValue=(string)($_POST['is_adult']??'0');
                if (!in_array($adultValue,['0','1'],true)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('+18 seçimi geçersiz.');
                }
                $isAdult=(int)$adultValue;

                $languageCode=trim((string)($_POST['language_code']??''));
                if ($languageCode!=='' && !array_key_exists($languageCode,$languageNames)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Dil seçimi geçersiz.');
                }
            }

            $tmdbId=null;
            $poster=null;
            $backdrop=null;
            $overview=null;
            $releaseDate=null;
            $releaseYear=null;
            $vote=0;
            $genres=null;
            $original=null;
            $logo=null;

            if ($type==='movie') {
                $tmdbId=(int)($_POST['tmdb_id']??0);
                $poster=trim((string)($_POST['poster_path']??''));
                $backdrop=trim((string)($_POST['backdrop_path']??''));
                $overview=trim((string)($_POST['overview']??''));
                $releaseDate=trim((string)($_POST['release_date']??''));
                $releaseYear=(int)($_POST['release_year']??0);
                $vote=(float)($_POST['vote_average']??0);
                $genres=trim((string)($_POST['genres']??''));
                $original=trim((string)($_POST['original_name']??''));
                $logo=trim((string)($_POST['logo_url']??''));

                if ($tmdbId<0||$releaseYear<0||$releaseYear>2200||
                    !is_finite($vote)||$vote<0||$vote>10) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Film bilgileri geçersiz.');
                }
                if (strlen($poster)>500||strlen($backdrop)>500||strlen($releaseDate)>20||
                    strlen($genres)>10000||strlen($original)>255||strlen($logo)>1000) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Film bilgileri izin verilen uzunluğu aşıyor.');
                }
                if ($logo!==''&&(!filter_var($logo,FILTER_VALIDATE_URL)||
                    !in_array(strtolower((string)parse_url($logo,PHP_URL_SCHEME)),['http','https'],true))) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Logo adresi HTTP veya HTTPS olmalı.');
                }
            }

            $values=[
                $category,$name,$url,$type,$status,$tmdbId,$poster,$backdrop,
                $overview,$releaseDate,$releaseYear,$vote,$genres,$original,$logo
            ];

            $pdo->beginTransaction();
            try {
                if ($id>0) {
                    $st=$pdo->prepare(
                        'UPDATE streams SET category_id=?,name=?,stream_url=?,type=?,status=?,
                        tmdb_id=?,poster_path=?,backdrop_path=?,overview=?,release_date=?,
                        release_year=?,vote_average=?,genres=?,original_name=?,logo_url=?
                        WHERE id=? AND type=?'
                    );
                    $st->execute([...$values,$id,$type]);

                    $check=$pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type=?');
                    $check->execute([$id,$type]);
                    if (!$check->fetch()) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Kayıt bulunamadı.');
                    }
                    $streamId=$id;
                } else {
                    $st=$pdo->prepare(
                        'INSERT INTO streams
                        (category_id,name,stream_url,type,status,tmdb_id,poster_path,backdrop_path,
                        overview,release_date,release_year,vote_average,genres,original_name,logo_url)
                        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)'
                    );
                    $st->execute($values);
                    $streamId=(int)$pdo->lastInsertId();
                }

                if ($type==='live') {
                    $adultUpdate=$pdo->prepare('UPDATE streams SET is_adult=? WHERE id=? AND type=\'live\'');
                    $adultUpdate->execute([$isAdult,$streamId]);

                    $languageUpdate=$pdo->prepare(
                        "UPDATE streams SET language_code=? WHERE id=? AND type='live'"
                    );
                    $languageUpdate->execute([
                        $languageCode!=='' ? $languageCode : null,
                        $streamId
                    ]);

                    $deletePackages=$pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM stream_packages WHERE stream_id=?');
                    $deletePackages->execute([$streamId]);

                    $insertPackage=$pdo->prepare(
                        'INSERT INTO stream_packages (stream_id,package_id) VALUES (?,?)'
                    );
                    foreach ($selectedPackageIds as $packageId) {
                        $insertPackage->execute([$streamId,$packageId]);
                    }
                }

                $pdo->commit();
            } catch (Throwable $saveError) {
                if ($pdo->inTransaction()) {
                    $pdo->rollBack();
                }
                throw $saveError;
            }

            $_SESSION['flash']=['success',$id>0
                ? $itemLabel.' bilgileri güncellendi.'
                : 'Yeni '.$itemLabel.' kaydedildi.'];
            contentGo($page.'#items');
        }

        if ($action==='stream_toggle') {
            $st=$pdo->prepare(
                "UPDATE streams SET status=IF(status='active','disabled','active')
                 WHERE id=? AND type=?"
            );
            $st->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0),$type]);
            $_SESSION['flash']=['success','Yayın durumu değiştirildi.'];
            contentGo($page.'#items');
        }

        if ($action==='stream_delete') {
            $st=$pdo->prepare('DELETE FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type=?');
            $st->execute([(int)($_POST['id']??0),$type]);
            $_SESSION['flash']=['success','Kayıt silindi.'];
            contentGo($page.'#items');
        }

        throw new RuntimeException('Bilinmeyen işlem.');
    } catch(PDOException $ex) {
        if ((string)$ex->getCode()==='23000') {
            $_SESSION['flash']=['error','İşlem tamamlanamadı. Kayıt başka içeriklerle bağlantılı olabilir.'];
        } else {
            error_log('Content panel database error: '.$ex->getMessage());
            $_SESSION['flash']=['error','Veritabanı işlemi tamamlanamadı.'];
        }
        contentGo($page);
    } catch(Throwable $ex) {
        $_SESSION['flash']=['error',$ex->getMessage()];
        contentGo($page);
    }
}

$flash=$_SESSION['flash']??null;
unset($_SESSION['flash']);

$editId=(int)($_GET['edit']??0);
$edit=null;
if ($editId>0) {
    $st=$pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM streams WHERE id=? AND type=?');
    $st->execute([$editId,$type]);
    $edit=$st->fetch()?:null;
}

$livePackages=[];
$editPackageIds=[];

if ($type==='live') {
    $livePackages=$pdo->query(
        "SELECT id,name FROM packages WHERE status='active' ORDER BY sort_order,id"
    )->fetchAll();

    if ($edit) {
        $st=$pdo->prepare('SELECT package_id FROM stream_packages WHERE stream_id=?');
        $st->execute([(int)$edit['id']]);
        $editPackageIds=array_map('intval',$st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));
    }
}

$editCategory=null;

if ($editCategoryId>0) {
    $st=$pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM categories WHERE id=? AND type=?');
    $st->execute([$editCategoryId,$type]);
    $editCategory=$st->fetch()?:null;
}

$categories=$pdo->prepare('SELECT * FROM categories WHERE type=? ORDER BY sort_order,name');
$categories->execute([$type]);
$categories=$categories->fetchAll();

$q=trim((string)($_GET['q']??''));
$languageFilter=trim((string)($_GET['language']??''));
$sql='SELECT s.*,c.name AS category_name FROM streams s
      LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id=s.category_id WHERE s.type=?';
$params=[$type];
if ($q!=='') {
    $sql.=' AND s.name LIKE ?';
    $params[]='%'.$q.'%';
}
if ($type==='live' && $languageFilter==='unknown') {
    $sql.=" AND (s.language_code IS NULL OR s.language_code='')";
} elseif ($type==='live' && array_key_exists($languageFilter,$languageNames)) {
    $sql.=' AND s.language_code=?';
    $params[]=$languageFilter;
} else {
    $languageFilter='';
}
$sql.=' ORDER BY s.id DESC LIMIT 300';
$st=$pdo->prepare($sql);
$st->execute($params);
$items=$st->fetchAll();

$total=(int)$st->fetchColumn();
$st=$pdo->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM streams WHERE type=? AND status='active'");
$st->execute([$type]);
$active=(int)$st->fetchColumn();
$categoryCount=count($categories);

<script>
function msg(t){document.getElementById('tmdbMessage').textContent=t}
function img(path,size='w500'){return path?'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/'+size+path:''}
async function searchTMDB(){
 const q=document.getElementById('tmdbQuery').value.trim();
 if(q.length<2){msg('En az 2 karakter yaz.');return}
 const btn=document.getElementById('tmdbSearchBtn'),box=document.getElementById('tmdbResults');
 btn.disabled=true;msg('TMDB aranıyor...');box.replaceChildren();
 try{
  const r=await fetch('tmdb.php?action=search&kind=movie&q='+encodeURIComponent(q),{credentials:'same-origin',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});
  const d=await r.json();if(!r.ok||!d.success)throw Error(d.error||'Arama başarısız.');
  if(!d.results?.length){msg('Sonuç bulunamadı.');return}
  msg(d.results.length+' sonuç bulundu. Filmi seç.');
  d.results.forEach(item=>{
   const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.style.cssText='text-align:left;background:#142237;border:1px solid #ffffff18;border-radius:10px;padding:8px;color:#fff;cursor:pointer;min-width:0';
   const image=document.createElement('img');image.src=img(item.poster_path,'w185');image.alt='';image.style.cssText='width:100%;height:150px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:7px;background:#0b1421';
   const title=document.createElement('div');title.textContent=item.title||'';title.style.cssText='font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-top:7px';
   const year=document.createElement('div');year.textContent=(item.release_date||'').slice(0,4);year.style.cssText='font-size:10px;color:#91a4bb;margin-top:4px';
   b.append(image,title,year);b.onclick=()=>selectTMDB(item.id);box.append(b);
  });
 }catch(e){msg(e.message||'TMDB bağlantı hatası.')}finally{btn.disabled=false}
}
async function selectTMDB(id){
 msg('Film bilgileri alınıyor...');
 try{
  const r=await fetch('tmdb.php?action=details&kind=movie&id='+encodeURIComponent(id),{credentials:'same-origin',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});
  const d=await r.json();if(!r.ok||!d.success)throw Error(d.error||'Bilgi alınamadı.');
  const x=d.item;
  document.getElementById('tmdbId').value=x.id||'';
  document.getElementById('posterPath').value=x.poster_path||'';
  document.getElementById('backdropPath').value=x.backdrop_path||'';
  document.getElementById('overview').value=x.overview||'';
  document.getElementById('releaseDate').value=x.release_date||'';
  document.getElementById('releaseYear').value=(x.release_date||'').slice(0,4);
  document.getElementById('voteAverage').value=x.vote_average||0;
  document.getElementById('genres').value=(x.genres||[]).map(g=>g.name).join(', ');
  document.getElementById('originalName').value=x.original_title||'';
  document.querySelector('input[name="name"]').value=x.title||'';
  document.getElementById('tmdbTitle').textContent=x.title||'';
  document.getElementById('tmdbInfo').textContent='Yıl: '+((x.release_date||'').slice(0,4)||'Bilinmiyor')+' · Puan: '+Number(x.vote_average||0).toFixed(1)+'/10 · '+(x.genres||[]).map(g=>g.name).join(', ');
  document.getElementById('tmdbOverview').textContent=x.overview||'Açıklama bulunmuyor.';
  const p=document.getElementById('tmdbPoster');p.src=img(x.poster_path);p.style.display=x.poster_path?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('tmdbSelected').style.display='block';document.getElementById('tmdbResults').replaceChildren();
  msg('Film seçildi. Yayın adresini ve kategoriyi kontrol ederek kaydedebilirsin.');
 }catch(e){msg(e.message||'Film bilgisi alınamadı.')}
}
document.getElementById('tmdbQuery').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();searchTMDB()}});
(function(){const id=document.getElementById('tmdbId').value;if(!id)return;const p=document.getElementById('posterPath').value;document.getElementById('tmdbTitle').textContent=document.querySelector('input[name="name"]').value;document.getElementById('tmdbInfo').textContent='TMDB ID: '+id+' · Yıl: '+document.getElementById('releaseYear').value+' · Puan: '+document.getElementById('voteAverage').value+'/10';document.getElementById('tmdbOverview').textContent=document.getElementById('overview').value;const image=document.getElementById('tmdbPoster');image.src=img(p);image.style.display=p?'block':'none';document.getElementById('tmdbSelected').style.display='block'})();
</script>

========== DOSYA: tmdb.php ==========
<?php
declare(strict_types=1);

ini_set('display_errors', '0');
error_reporting(E_ALL);

session_name('XTREAM_ADMIN');
session_set_cookie_params([
    'lifetime'=>28800,
    'path'=>'/',
    'secure'=>true,
    'httponly'=>true,
    'samesite'=>'Strict'
]);
session_start();

header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
header('Cache-Control: no-store');
header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');

function respond(array $data, int $status=200): never {
    http_response_code($status);
    echo json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE|JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES);
    exit;
}

if (empty($_SESSION['admin_id'])) {
    respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'Yönetici girişi gerekli.'],401);
}

if (!extension_loaded('curl')) {
    respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'PHP cURL eklentisi yüklü değil.'],500);
}

$config=@parse_ini_file('/etc/xtream-panel/tmdb.ini');
$key=$config['TMDB_API_KEY']??'';

if (!is_string($key)||trim($key)==='') {
    respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'TMDB API ayarı bulunamadı.'],500);
}

function tmdbRequest(string $path, array $params=[]): array {
    global $key;

    $params['api_key']= [GIZLENDI]
    $params['language']='tr-TR';

    $url='https://api.themoviedb.org/3/'.$path.'?'.http_build_query($params);

    $ch=curl_init($url);
    curl_setopt_array($ch,[
        CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER=>true,
        CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION=>false,
        CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT=>8,
        CURLOPT_TIMEOUT=>15,
        CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER=>true,
        CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST=>2,
        CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER=>['Accept: application/json']
    ]);

    $body=curl_exec($ch);
    $status=(int)curl_getinfo($ch,CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
    curl_close($ch);

    if ($body===false||$status!==200) {
        throw new RuntimeException('TMDB servisine ulaşılamadı. HTTP '.$status);
    }

    $data=json_decode($body,true);
    if (!is_array($data)) {
        throw new RuntimeException('TMDB yanıtı okunamadı.');
    }

    return $data;
}

try {
    $action=(string)($_GET['action']??'');
    $kind=(string)($_GET['kind']??'');

    if ($action==='search') {
        $query=trim((string)($_GET['q']??''));

        if (!in_array($kind,['movie','tv'],true)) {
            respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'Arama türü geçersiz.'],400);
        }
        if (mb_strlen($query)<2||mb_strlen($query)>100) {
            respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'En az 2, en fazla 100 karakter girin.'],400);
        }

        $path=$kind==='movie'?'search/movie':'search/tv';
        $data=tmdbRequest($path,[
            'query'=>$query,
            'include_adult'=>'false',
            'page'=>1
        ]);

        $results=[];
        foreach (($data['results']??[]) as $item) {
            $results[]=[
                'id'=>$item['id']??null,
                'title'=>$item['title']??$item['name']??'',
                'original_title'=>$item['original_title']??$item['original_name']??'',
                'overview'=>$item['overview']??'',
                'poster_path'=>$item['poster_path']??null,
                'backdrop_path'=>$item['backdrop_path']??null,
                'release_date'=>$item['release_date']??$item['first_air_date']??'',
                'vote_average'=>$item['vote_average']??0,
                'genre_ids'=>$item['genre_ids']??[]
            ];
        }

        respond(['success'=>true,'results'=>$results]);
    }

    if ($action==='details') {
        if (!in_array($kind,['movie','tv'],true)) {
            respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'İçerik türü geçersiz.'],400);
        }

        $id=filter_input(INPUT_GET,'id',FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        if (!$id||$id<1) {
            respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'Geçerli TMDB numarası gerekli.'],400);
        }

        $path=($kind==='movie'?'movie/':'tv/').$id;
        $item=tmdbRequest($path);

        respond([
            'success'=>true,
            'item'=>[
                'id'=>$item['id']??null,
                'title'=>$item['title']??$item['name']??'',
                'original_title'=>$item['original_title']??$item['original_name']??'',
                'overview'=>$item['overview']??'',
                'poster_path'=>$item['poster_path']??null,
                'backdrop_path'=>$item['backdrop_path']??null,
                'release_date'=>$item['release_date']??$item['first_air_date']??'',
                'vote_average'=>$item['vote_average']??0,
                'genres'=>$item['genres']??[],
                'number_of_seasons'=>$item['number_of_seasons']??0,
                'number_of_episodes'=>$item['number_of_episodes']??0
            ]
        ]);
    }

    if ($action==='season') {
        $id=filter_input(INPUT_GET,'id',FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $season=filter_input(INPUT_GET,'season',FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

        if (!$id||$id<1||$season===false||$season===null||$season<0) {
            respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'Dizi veya sezon numarası geçersiz.'],400);
        }

        $item=tmdbRequest('tv/'.$id.'/season/'.$season);

        $episodes=[];
        foreach (($item['episodes']??[]) as $ep) {
            $episodes[]=[
                'id'=>$ep['id']??null,
                'episode_number'=>$ep['episode_number']??0,
                'name'=>$ep['name']??'',
                'overview'=>$ep['overview']??'',
                'still_path'=>$ep['still_path']??null,
                'air_date'=>$ep['air_date']??'',
                'vote_average'=>$ep['vote_average']??0,
                'runtime'=>$ep['runtime']??null
            ];
        }

        respond([
            'success'=>true,
            'season'=>[
                'id'=>$item['id']??null,
                'season_number'=>$item['season_number']??$season,
                'name'=>$item['name']??'',
                'overview'=>$item['overview']??'',
                'poster_path'=>$item['poster_path']??null,
                'air_date'=>$item['air_date']??'',
                'episodes'=>$episodes
            ]
        ]);
    }

    respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>'Geçersiz işlem.'],400);

} catch(Throwable $e) {
    respond(['success'=>false,'error'=>$e->getMessage()],502);
}
